The eight Wave-5 bites are the opening record (`T11-041`), which rules the decisions and discharges no implementation deliverable; the STATE record and structural fragments (`T11-042`), which discharge deliverables 1, 2, 3, 10 and 11 and the record half of 13; semantic fragments (`T11-043`), which discharge deliverables 4–8; the operator fragment and membership producer (`T11-044`), which discharge deliverable 9 and the operator relation of exit 2; the composed program, production subtree and constructor re-run (`T11-045`), which discharge deliverables 12–14 and exits 1, 3 and 4; assessment and carrier projection (`T11-046`), which complete exit 2; adversarial closure tests (`T11-047`), which reconfirm all fourteen deliverables and four exits; and closure and handoff (`T11-048`), which records the Wave-6 unresolved-symbol handoff, Wave-7 membership-byte handoff and deferred Wave-8 and Wave-12 evidence. `T11-042` can start after this record lands, and `T11-044`'s producer can start after `T11-042`'s record type exists; nothing is handed up because every decision was decidable on the merits.

31. The announcement leaf cannot push its own static-subtree root as a consumer because the production subtree is that one leaf. The root is therefore a witness item bound to the introspected program by the tweak relation and retained for the successor; because the reviewed registry's stack primitives reach only three items deep, copy-through carries the retained root (`0.6.182-dev`).

32. The tapscript prerequisite adapter omits the streaming-hash opcodes and the non-opcode execution and resource capabilities, while the composed record carries no transaction-header or outpoint inspection. Transaction-header inspection, streaming hash and root effects therefore remain pattern-required in the record-backed assessment until a later bite extends the adapter (`0.6.184-dev`).

33. The constructibility relation carries operator evidence in the plan and therefore maps to realization's external requirement, while authorization is emitted by the operator fragment. The complete projection consequently has 24 emitted carriers and two external carriers (`0.6.184-dev`).

34. The reviewed target charges no operation cost, so the resource projection's operation dimension is zero by construction; the composed record's bound checks bite on its 2,243 script bytes and 150 validation units (`0.6.183-dev`).

35. Selected sighash semantics and the operator-authorization capability remain missing while their registry entries are unreviewed, whatever components the record carries (`0.6.184-dev`).

36. The closure tests record what the public tree does not expose rather than claiming it away: authentication and copy-through slice their inputs, so the abstract walk's declared precondition establishes full item widths rather than a run-time check on every item; recipe constructors refuse missing components before producing metadata, so subset unions are not observable; the compiler's corruption accessors are private to it, so duplicate-relation corruption is unreachable from tapscript; ten refusal variants — structural and composed invalid-contract, the program and contract variants of the semantic and operator records, the composed program, resource-limit and subtree variants, and the carrier projection's representation disagreement — are unreachable through admitted inputs and need internal contract, assembly, subtree and projection seams; every projected resource dimension is unbounded on this target, so the builder's resource refusal cannot be shown to bite while the independent stack bound can; and the constructor fixture's scripted curve injects a fixed output key, so the real curve result is verified beside it. Each gap is typed evidence for Wave 6 to open as a follow-up row or decline on the merits (`0.6.185-dev`).

37. The maintained plans weight stands at 1024799 bytes against the 1,062,912-byte soft target after this wave, so a plans-weight decision — raising the target or splitting the backlog — is due before the Wave-6 opening. That decision was taken as ruling 42, both measures together, and landed through `0.6.187-dev`.

### Wave-6 plan findings · `sec:phase6:wave6-findings`

The Wave-6 opening study was a read-only source study with no gate run, and every citation was self-audited; the study's totals are recorded here as the wave's baseline rather than as a claim about the reduced leaf.

38. The bound of 64 distinct references shared by the constructor's local census and the linker's frozen graph is finding 24 at (`sec:phase6:wave4-findings`), not ruling 24, whose subject is the closed `StateStaticSubtree` type and the first Wave-5 bite (`rule:phase6:wave4-rulings`); the Wave-6 study's brief attributed the bound to the ruling, and the attribution is corrected here on file evidence without changing the bound, which stands at 64 for the admitted graph (`packages/linker/src/state_constructor_graph.rs:18`).
39. The wave's baseline census was recounted at the Wave-5 closure tip by translating the emitters into an independent recount rather than by copying the closure record, and it agrees with it: twelve distinct program symbols over 46 push sites in the composed announcement leaf. The structural components carry 33 of those sites — `StateAsset` 7, `StateAmount` 1, `PredecessorProgram` 1, `FeeSponsorInputMax` 3, `ReserveAsset` 12, `SponsorChangeProgram` 3, `SponsorChangeVersion` 3 and `FeeProgramDigest` 3 — because three physical partitions are emitted rather than four, the sponsor-isolation component sharing the cardinality component's range, and recognition adds the asset, amount and predecessor program once each (`packages/tapscript/src/state_pattern.rs:511-600`; `packages/tapscript/src/state_program.rs:264-281`). The semantic components carry twelve — `InternalKey` 4, `MaturityLeadMin` 3, `MaturityLeadMax` 3, and one site each for the asset and amount they share into the structural identities, which takes those two symbols to 8 and 2 over the whole leaf — and the operator component carries one, `CommittedOperatorKey` (`packages/tapscript/src/state_announcement.rs:380-539`; `packages/tapscript/src/state_operator.rs:42-45`). The announcement's realization relations number 26, of which 24 have emitted carriers and two remain external (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/maturity_assessment_tests.rs:1058-1066`). Ruling 41 takes the census to eleven symbols over 45 sites and ruling 40 takes it to six over a count the reduction bite recounts and publishes; every figure recorded here is a baseline, not a promise.
40. Wave-6 scope and the ABI handoff are stated rather than assumed: this wave publishes three-sided carrier closure — compiler, emitted and linked — with an exact contract for the fourth side, the guide's four-sided statement at (`rule:guide14-exec:carrier-closure`) receives its staged interpretation on this card under ruling 48, and Wave 7 supplies the ABI side against the same keys (`task:guide14-exec:wave7`). Nothing is declared discharged by prose.
41. The evidence behind ruling 40 is three facts and one collapse: with the executing input pinned to index zero by `StateCoordinatorRoleV1`, the singleton declared with a fixed amount of one and not reissuable, and target consensus conserving each asset per transaction, no position after zero can carry or affect the singleton, so the announcement leaf's exact counts, sponsor-suffix sweep, committed change program and version, fee digest, sponsor-maximum literal and issuance sweep protect nothing those three facts do not already protect (`packages/tapscript/src/state_pattern.rs:435-443`; `packages/architecture/src/spec.rs:489-498`; `packages/tapscript/src/state_pattern.rs:98-117`). Their remaining purpose was excluding other operations from the transaction, which the compositional argument supplies without them, so the four absence facts of `StateStructuralEvidence` collapse to one — that no second STATE object occurs — discharged by the pin, by conservation and by two named external evidence roles, the non-reissuable declaration and the singleton's issuance under the constructor; the other three said only that no position remained for another family, which was true because the leaf had claimed every position (`packages/tapscript/src/state_pattern.rs:121-140`).
42. `SponsorEnvelopeMultiplicity { maximum }` is resource policy rather than semantics, because the compiler models sponsorship as `SponsorCase::{Absent, Present}`, two values and not a count (`packages/compiler/src/case.rs:46-49`); whether the realization retains the relation is a realization decision left open here, and the leaf carries no bound for it meanwhile, because under ruling 52 a transaction-wide count bound is itself a surplus check once composition is permitted.
43. The committed sponsor-change program over-approximated its property: the property was that output one is not a family object, while the check was equality against one exact witness program and version, which also fixes where a sponsor's change may go as a condition of announcing maturity (`packages/tapscript/src/state_pattern.rs:541-551`). It disappears with the reduction, and the observation is recorded so that no later family repeats the shape.
44. The structural predecessor literal was a regression against the estate's own earlier answers: the compact generation's linker refuses a self-committing literal outright as `ImpossibleStaticFixedPoint` and records `SelfCommitmentStrategy::IdentityIntrospection` as the resolution, and the live generation made recognition strictly local for the same reason (`packages/linker/src/graph.rs:493-537`; `packages/linker/src/deployment.rs:40-57`). Ruling 41 puts STATE on the same footing, which is the third time this question has been answered the same way.
45. Plans weight: the archive of the 103 closed rows and the raise of the soft target to 1,200 KiB landed together through `0.6.187-dev`, where the maintained tree measures 883,525 bytes against the new 1,228,800-byte target — 345,275 bytes of headroom — where it measured 1,024,799 bytes before the move, and the constant's own documentation carries those figures (`packages/labels/src/plans.rs:104`). The archive's arithmetic closes: 141,507 bytes of rows left section 11 and a 233-byte pointer paragraph entered it, a net 141,274 bytes, which is exactly the difference between the two measurements. A recount at the tip this record opens from reads 883,881 bytes, 356 more, which is the plans README paragraph the raise landed beside the constant; finding 37's decision is thereby taken.
46. Two Phase-6 obligations are opened as rows rather than left implicit: the extension-point notes at the four sites where a second constructor generation would enter, which `T11-062` closes only on four diff citations, and the audit of the compact and live generations against the closure-exact principle, which `T11-063` schedules after the Phase-6 closure unless a Wave-6 link depends on it.
47. Ruling 40 reaches the realization: five of the announcement's relations — `AllowedObjectFamilies` on each side, `SponsorIsolation`, `CanonicalDeltaPolicy`, `OpenFlowPolicy` and `SponsorEnvelopeMultiplicity` — are evaluated over the whole observed transaction rather than over the positions the operation claims (`packages/realization/src/evaluate.rs:414-493`), so with composition permitted the model would refuse what the covenant accepts. Ruling 52 re-scopes them to the operation's own region by a staged refit under `T11-064`, and the discharge table classes them MODEL-SCOPE until that row lands.

The wave's figures are recounted here from the tree, each from the test or constant that fixes it, rather than carried forward from the bites that reported them. Finding 39's baseline of twelve distinct program symbols over 46 push sites — 33 structural, twelve semantic and one operator — meets a reduced leaf of six symbols over fifteen sites: `StateAsset` at two sites, `StateAmount` at two, `InternalKey` at four, `MaturityLeadMin` at three, `MaturityLeadMax` at three and `CommittedOperatorKey` at one, read from the per-symbol map `the_consumer_census_is_six_keys_over_fifteen_sites_and_seven_fields` asserts (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_symbol_tests.rs`) and agreed independently over the composed record's own consumers by `the_composed_consumer_census_is_exactly_six_symbols_over_fifteen_sites` (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_program_tests.rs`). The structural identity census fell from five to two: `StatePatternId` now holds `StateCoordinatorRoleV1` and `StateInputRecognitionV1`, where `0.6.189-dev` removed `StateCardinalityV1`, `StateSponsorIsolationV1` and `StateIssuanceAbsenceV1`. The link resolves thirteen closed keys over five typed origins — the six program symbols above and seven constructor-reference kinds — two of them from the deployment, one from the architecture's asset declaration, two from the architecture's lead bounds, seven from the constructor and one from the reviewed target, read from `pass_one_defines_thirteen_keys_over_five_typed_origins` beside `the_census_is_thirteen_keys_in_key_order_six_of_them_pushed`. The relocation census is fifteen records, of which nine move under the second deployment and six do not, asserted over linked bytes by `a_second_deployment_moves_only_the_sites_whose_values_moved` (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`) and over the linker's own records by `the_second_deployment_moves_nine_sites_and_nothing_else` (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_bundle_tests.rs`). The committed tree costs zero statically and two complete, recomputed by the subset recurrence and by the equal-weight and enumerated routes in `the_cost_figures_are_recomputed_by_independent_routes` beside `the_singleton_tree_carries_one_leaf_at_the_root`. The linked resource evidence is five measured dimensions — script bytes, validation budget, initial stack items, peak stack items, and an operation cost of zero — with five more absent rather than zero and no saturated figure among them, each compared with the source that fixes it rather than with a written-down number, and the second deployment's totals equal the first's: `the_checked_totals_are_the_linked_program_s_own`, `the_peak_stack_is_the_walk_s_own_combined_depth`, `the_second_deployment_costs_what_the_first_does`, and `the_final_stack_walk_is_empty_on_both_sides_of_the_link` over the linked program and the pristine one it was substituted from. The discharge table is 26 relations over 104 rows — two representations by two execution cases — of which 96 are active and eight vacuous over four sponsor-family relations, and its class census is thirteen emitted, one deployment, ten model-scope and two external, asserted by `the_closure_has_one_row_per_relation_representation_and_case`, `the_class_census_is_the_records_own_discharge_table` and `a_vacuous_case_carries_nothing_and_claims_nothing` (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_carrier_tests.rs`) and read again from the closure over linked bytes by `every_emitted_discharge_is_found_where_the_closure_claims_it`. The outstanding ABI contract is 38 obligations, nineteen per representation and equal across the two, in `the_outstanding_contract_is_derived_keyed_and_equal_across_representations`, and the outstanding link obligations are the five constant members of `OutstandingStateLinkObligations`, checked by `the_status_is_a_prototype_and_the_outstanding_set_is_the_five`. The closed refusal root `StateLinkRefusal` holds 50 variants walked by an exhaustive match in `the_closed_refusal_root_is_covered_by_test_or_by_reason`, so a variant added to it fails to compile until a test reaches it or a recomputed reason says nothing can. The reduced leaf's kept checks are the thirteen members of `KeptCheck::ALL`, and the golden is six pinned constants compared with the recomputation and with what the bundle carries in one comparison by `the_golden_roots_are_pinned_recomputed_and_carried_alike`, the demonstration deployment's static root and outer root equal to the constants two independent curve implementations agree on in `packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_constructor_tests.rs`. No instruction count or byte total for the leaf is recounted here, because the tree pins none; finding 65 records why.

Row `T11-052` is DONE on `0.6.189-dev`. Its gate asked that absent semantic authentication reject, that a wrong actual predecessor fail the authenticated equation, that no predecessor literal survive, that a transaction carrying extra inputs and outputs of any asset but the singleton be accepted, and that the singleton at any position other than input zero or output zero be refused; the tests that carry it are `authentication_rejects_every_metadata_byte_and_wrong_parity` and `a_wrong_predecessor_program_fails_the_authenticated_equation` (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_announcement_tests.rs`), `a_predecessor_program_literal_that_returns_is_refused` over the linked bytes and the four forbidden literals its census compares them against (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`), `the_composed_leaf_observes_only_position_zero_so_other_positions_are_free`, which walks every introspection in the composed leaf and finds a literal zero before each, and the two refused singleton cases of `the_four_adoption_vectors_are_decided_by_the_leafs_own_bytes` beside `the_accepted_vector_carries_foreign_positions_the_leaf_never_names`, whose accepted shape carries three inputs, four outputs and a fee output that is not last.

Row `T11-053` is DONE on `0.6.190-dev`. Its gate asked that a missing or unvalidated source refuse by type and that both external evidence roles appear in the deployment binding; `bind_refuses_an_identity_other_than_the_operators` and `binding_returns_every_supplied_source_and_the_reviewed_revision` carry the first, and `both_singleton_facts_appear_in_the_binding_and_freshness_does_not` beside `the_deployment_facts_are_exactly_the_three_deployment_side_roles` carry the second, the freshness role staying out because no deployment records it (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_deployment_tests.rs`).

Row `T11-054` is DONE on `0.6.192-dev`. Its gate asked that every definition key match an actual record site or a constructor-field consumer before normalization, that a constructor-policy consumer count even without a script push, and that the census closed over be the reduced one; `the_census_is_thirteen_keys_in_key_order_six_of_them_pushed`, `pass_one_defines_thirteen_keys_over_five_typed_origins` and `the_consumer_census_is_six_keys_over_fifteen_sites_and_seven_fields` carry the census, the last asserting that exactly the seven non-pushed keys are constructor fields, while `a_consumed_key_with_no_definition_is_refused` and `a_definition_no_consumer_reads_is_refused` walk the closure statement one key at a time in both directions and `a_definition_of_the_wrong_kind_is_refused` separates width from meaning.

Row `T11-055` is DONE on `0.6.193-dev`. Its gate is `G14C-08` and `G14C-09` on ruling 50's criteria and on nothing weaker, dispositioned below; beside those, `the_frozen_projection_agrees_with_the_constructor_kind_nodes` holds the constructor projection to the seven kind nodes and `the_shared_reference_bound_admits_sixty_four_nodes_and_no_more` holds the admitted graph to the shared bound.

Row `T11-056` is DONE on `0.6.194-dev`. Its gate asked for `G14C-10`'s stable-identity, permutation and simultaneous-rebuild contribution, dispositioned below; the resource half is carried by `the_checked_totals_are_the_linked_program_s_own`, `the_observations_partition_the_capability_roster`, which leaves no capability both present and absent, `every_named_gap_is_a_fact_of_the_emitters`, which recomputes each named gap from the emitter it comes from rather than asserting it, and `no_admissible_program_can_overflow_a_checked_total`.

Row `T11-057` is DONE on `0.6.191-dev`. Its gate asked that static cost be zero and the unit-weight complete tree cost two, that the evidence bind to the exact linked leaf bytes, and that a deployment depth cap be enforced rather than assumed; `the_singleton_tree_carries_one_leaf_at_the_root` and `the_cost_figures_are_recomputed_by_independent_routes` carry the figures, `the_binding_is_to_bytes_rather_than_to_a_role_list` and `a_declaration_the_constructor_did_not_commit_is_refused` carry the binding, and `the_depth_cap_is_read_over_the_complete_tree` carries the cap over the complete tree rather than over the static subtree alone.

Row `T11-058` is DONE on `0.6.195-dev`. Its gate asked that representation, execution case, relation, discharge boundary and selected alternative be compared, that vacuity and external requirements be preserved, that actual component ranges be verified inside the committed leaf, that duplicate, missing, extra and unreachable assignments be refused in both directions, and that the model-scope rows name `T11-064`; `the_closure_has_one_row_per_relation_representation_and_case` and `every_row_is_reachable_by_its_complete_key` carry the keying, `every_emitted_row_sits_at_its_range_in_the_committed_linked_leaf` carries the ranges, `a_vacuous_case_carries_nothing_and_claims_nothing` and `a_component_claiming_a_vacuous_case_is_refused` carry vacuity from both sides, `an_open_premise_that_disappears_is_refused` and `every_open_premise_of_the_plan_is_visible_on_some_row` carry the external requirements, `a_relation_the_record_answers_for_nowhere_is_refused` and `a_carrier_for_a_relation_the_plan_does_not_carry_is_refused` carry the two directions, and `the_model_scope_rows_are_the_ten_no_leaf_carries` carries the model-scope set.

Row `T11-059` is DONE on `0.6.196-dev`. Its gate asked that the §17.3 continuity equality be one named check and that supplied concrete instances be retained only with their exact metadata; `two_bundles_static_subtrees_do_not_migrate` carries the equality with both roots on the refusal, `an_application_is_metadata_specific_over_one_fixed_subtree` and `retaining_one_metadata_twice_is_refused` carry the retention, and `the_applied_census_moves_the_static_root_and_nothing_else` and `an_applied_reference_that_is_not_the_handed_subtree_is_refused` hold the application to the linked subtree it was handed.

Row `T11-060` is DONE on `0.6.197-dev` for its node-free half and `0.6.198-dev` for its native half. Its gate asked that a test reading only linked bytes fail if a removed check returns or a kept check is absent; `a_kept_check_that_goes_missing_is_refused_by_name` deletes each of the thirteen kept checks from the decoded bytes in turn and `a_removed_primitive_that_returns_is_refused_by_name` inserts each removed primitive, with `the_reduced_leaf_carries_every_kept_check_and_no_removed_primitive`, `an_introspection_that_names_no_literal_zero_is_refused` and `a_consumer_site_the_bytes_do_not_push_is_refused` completing the census, all over bytes that `both_deployments_link_and_their_linked_bytes_decode_to_the_records_shape` re-decodes from the link. The native half is `the_four_adoption_vectors_are_submitted_to_a_real_target`, which is ignored by default and requires the native adapter and a target node, so its evidence is the run recorded with that landing rather than an assertion of the ordinary gate.

`G14C-08` closes on ruling 50's criterion — a residual-DAG proof together with a component carrying two edge-disjoint cycles where one authenticated cut is insufficient and the linker must refuse — and on nothing weaker. `the_three_cuts_are_removed_and_the_residual_is_acyclic` removes the three validated cuts, checks that each carries required evidence, and leaves sixteen residual edges in sixteen acyclic components; `the_dependency_order_places_every_referent_before_its_referrer` derives the canonical order and checks it against every residual edge; and the negative `one_cut_leaves_the_other_edge_disjoint_cycle` presents a component with two edge-disjoint cycles and one authenticated cut and reads back a residual-cycle refusal naming the component, its two cyclic edges and the single cut removed, with `a_cut_on_each_cycle_assembles` and `one_cut_on_an_edge_both_cycles_share_assembles` as the positives that keep the refusal from being a blanket one (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_graph_tests.rs`, `0.6.193-dev`).

`G14C-09` closes on ruling 50's criterion — closed binding-time forms, the refusal of a literal static root beneath itself, and exact witnessed-root authentication. The forms are the five members of `StateBindingTime` with the three that resolve a cycle named by `resolves_a_cycle` and their required evidence fixed by `exactly_the_cutting_binding_times_state_required_evidence` and `the_record_shows_exactly_what_the_cut_rows_require`; the literal refusal is `a_link_time_static_root_under_its_own_program_is_refused`, which offers the literal beside a live cycle so that the refusal read back is the literal one rather than whichever check ran first, with `a_witnessed_root_without_the_root_witness_is_not_a_cut` refusing a cut whose witnesses are incomplete and `the_witnessed_root_carries_no_relocation_and_no_pushable_item` refusing ordinary link-time relocation of that symbol; and the authentication is exact in `authentication_retains_exact_witnessed_root_beneath_metadata`, `wrong_witnessed_root_and_internal_key_do_not_authenticate` and `a_wrong_predecessor_program_fails_the_authenticated_equation` (`0.6.189-dev`, `0.6.193-dev`).

`G14C-10` closes on ruling 50's criterion — stable definition, edge, component, relocation and equal-cost-tree identities with simultaneous pristine rebuilding and reparsing under permutations — with one clause vacuous and the vacuity stated rather than passed over. Edge identity is `the_real_graph_is_the_written_edge_table` and edge-order independence `the_declaration_order_does_not_reach_the_graph`; component identity is read back on the refusal in `one_cut_leaves_the_other_edge_disjoint_cycle`; relocation identity is one record per occurrence in `the_demonstration_census_has_one_record_per_occurrence` with `every_demonstration_record_names_the_component_that_holds_its_site` and `the_component_ranges_tile_the_whole_program`; the simultaneous rebuild is `the_census_and_the_rebuild_are_independent_of_input_order`, which resolves the census from reversed input, rebuilds in both symbol orders and reaches the same linked program that one rebuild with every value together reaches, while the reparse is the round trip every substitution runs and whose refusal the closed root declares unreachable with the reason that the encoder writes the minimal form the parser accepts; leaf permutation is `reversing_the_declarations_assembles_the_same_tree`. The equal-cost-tie clause has no subject in this generation: the ruled static tree is the singleton whose cost is zero, with one leaf and no topology to choose in `the_singleton_tree_carries_one_leaf_at_the_root`, and `a_declaration_the_constructor_did_not_commit_is_refused` keeps a second candidate from entering, so there is no candidate set for a tie to arise in. A clause with no subject is met rather than evaded, and what would re-arm it is named: a generation whose static tree admits equal-cost candidates re-opens the clause for that generation (`0.6.191-dev`, `0.6.193-dev`, `0.6.194-dev`).

`T11-062` does not close with this record. Three of its four sites are in the tree and citable as diffs of their landings: `StateConstructorGeneration` carries the note that a generation names a recipe rather than a version of a datum inside one, that the census holds a single member because §17.3 admits no migration and none is implemented, and that the guide's own status lists migration among the outstanding items (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs`, `0.6.189-dev`); the STATE graph's binding-time enum carries the note that a reference to a previously linked bundle would be a backward, acyclic edge — a resolution class and not a cut — named so that `WitnessedThenAuthenticated` is not mistaken for it and with no variant minted for it, citing §17.3 as where such an edge would first appear (`packages/linker/src/state_graph.rs`, `0.6.193-dev`); and `state_bundle_continuity` carries the note that it is the no-migration rule written once, the single assertion a migration relation would replace, deciding nothing about how such a generation would be shaped (`packages/linker/src/state_bundle.rs`, `0.6.196-dev`). Two gaps remain against the row's own criterion. `ReferenceClass` in `packages/linker/src/graph.rs`, which the row names first for site 2, carries no such paragraph: its documentation is the classification note it already had, and `0.6.193-dev` does not touch that file. `MetadataAuthentication` states the current shape's reason and that generation support would enter through its authentication against the prior generation's recipe, but cites the guide nowhere, where the other three notes cite §17.3 in the idiom the repository's Rust sources use (`packages/tapscript/src/state_announcement.rs`, `0.6.189-dev`). The row therefore stays open at those two points and closes under a following documentation step rather than under this record, while `T11-051` closes here, its rulings, findings, rows and register entry all landed.

Wave 7 receives the fourth carrier side against the same keys at (`task:guide14-exec:wave7`): the 38 obligations of the outstanding contract, nineteen per representation and equal across the two, each keyed like a closure row and naming a witness role, a slot, a deployment fact or an open premise, so the ABI answers one relation at a time rather than in bulk; and four of the five outstanding link obligations — that the current state was not validated against an observed predecessor, that no representation nonce was searched for a successor of one, that nothing is finalized and no witness populated, and that the ABI is unsupplied. These are the design's obligations rather than omissions: a link handed semantic metadata and a constructor makes no observation of a chain, and a candidate that finalized a spend would have stopped being a candidate. The fifth, that the relations the realization evaluates over a whole observed transaction are enforced by no leaf, is the realization's refit under `T11-064` and is handed there.

Wave 8 receives the native obligations at (`task:guide14-exec:wave8`), where the target-native rows of §9.3's evidence census already sit. What the wave's run establishes is that a target's own interpreter accepts the whole chain from the linked leaf's bytes through the static subtree, the output key and the control block, reaching the leaf's first instruction pair and refusing there at the committed operator key; what it cannot establish is the adoption gate's accepted case, and the three facts in the way are facts of the deployment rather than of the run: the demonstration deployment commits an operator key whose scalar nobody holds while the leaf verifies a signature under it, the pinned singleton is a fixture constant while a node derives asset identity from its own issuing outpoint, and output zero's program is authenticated against witness-supplied successor metadata that the bundle's outstanding set says nothing here populates. The enabling step is therefore a parameterization of the vectors' sources by singleton asset and committed operator key, so that a deployment can commit a test signer's key and a node-issued asset and the gate's discrimination can be exercised end to end rather than observed up to the signature.

Wave 12 receives the resource obligations at (`task:guide14-exec:wave12`): the six measured dimensions and the five absent ones as the exact linked predictions its target observations are compared against, the operation cost of zero that the reviewed target charges by construction, the committed tree's static and complete costs bound to the linked leaf's bytes, and the named capability gaps recomputed from their emitters rather than covered — the streaming-hash primitives the prerequisite adapter assigns no capability to, and the per-opcode constants the reviewed contract charges a hash by. Each is a measurement this wave declined to invent, which is what leaves the later observation something to disagree with.

48. The deployment depth cap arrives at `StateLinkDeploymentParameters::bind` as a bare `NonZeroU32` while every other source in the same call is a validated type carrying its own origin, so the one bound the deployment supplies is the one the census cannot say where it came from (`packages/linker/src/state_deployment.rs`).
49. `InvalidDefinitionItem` exists only because stack-item construction is fallible at the census boundary: the definition items are eight or thirty-two bytes and the reviewed literal bound is far above both, so the variant is a consequence of the constructor's signature rather than a reachable failure, which is why the closed root declares it unreachable rather than reaching it (`packages/linker/src/state_symbol.rs`).
50. Two test-only `ScriptedCurve` stubs remained after the taptree and symbol imports, one in the shared maturity fixtures and one local to the taptree tests, where one shared fixture would do; the relocate tests' own module documentation assigns unifying the maturity fixtures to the wave's closure bite, and a documentation-only closure record leaves it to the next step that touches that code (`packages/linker/src/tests/mod.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_taptree_tests.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_relocate_tests.rs`).
51. `StateStaticSubtree::new` admits a subtree committing one role at several identities — the repeated-role fixture validates as a subtree — and the refusal comes later from the constructor as `ExecutingLeafRepeated`, so the type's own admission is wider than the link's, which is why the binding's census check is an independent statement of the same rule rather than a path a derived constructor can reach (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_taptree_tests.rs`).
52. `LinkRefusal` implements neither `Display` nor `Error`: its only implementation is the conversion from the STATE root, so a caller outside the workspace cannot print or chain a link refusal without matching it (`packages/linker/src/error.rs`).
53. The constructor exposes no metadata-leaf-hash accessor, so consumers recompute it from `leaf_program()` and the leaf-hash function the closure imports; the vectors closure does exactly that when it checks the announcement leaf against the bundle's static root (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`).
54. `SuccessorReconstruction` reads no nonce and `CopyThrough` does, so the in-program reconstruction row names both components: the reconstruction reads the root and the successor's key prefix, while the nonce that fixes the successor's representation is read by the copy-through component whose derived metadata that reconstruction authenticates, and dropping the nonce would admit a reconstruction over metadata whose representation nobody witnessed (`packages/linker/src/state_graph.rs`).
55. `capability_of` yields no capability two ways — an explicit arm for the three streaming-hash primitives and a wildcard for a primitive this version has not been taught — and only the first is a recomputable gap for this leaf, because the leaf schedules those primitives while it schedules nothing untaught; the resource module names the three in a constant and recomputes the gap from them rather than asserting it (`packages/tapscript/src/pattern.rs`, `packages/linker/src/state_resource.rs`).
56. The demonstration fixtures resolve every key to the values the record was composed against, so the demonstration link is the identity on bytes and cannot tell a substitution from a copy; the second deployment, which differs in the asset, the operator key and both lead bounds and agrees in the internal key and the amount, is what makes substitution observable, and every closure test runs over both (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_relocate_tests.rs`).
57. The discharge table classes ten relation identities model-scope where ruling 52 names five relations: those five are declared over six identities, the family closure once per transaction side, and they are the region-scoping refit's scope; the remaining four — plain-L-BTC cardinality and recognition on each side — lost their carrier with the fragment that had claimed every position and are model-scope for that reason rather than the refit's (`the_model_scope_rows_are_the_ten_no_leaf_carries`).
58. `StateProgramWitness` derives no `Ord`, so witness sets are ordered by another key or kept as sequences: the outstanding contract carries its requirements as a vector and the evidence attribution is walked as pairs, each with the reason stated where the ordering would otherwise have been assumed (`packages/tapscript/src/state_program.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_carrier_tests.rs`).
59. Thirteen kept checks, not twelve: the metadata authentication's script-version equality is its own site, so `KeptCheck::ALL` separates the predecessor's program version from the predecessor tweak it sits inside, and folding the two would let one go missing while the other still matched (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`).
60. Distinct located components are at most the emitted relation count rather than equal to it, because several relations share one component, so the located census is compared class by class against the closure's own rather than component by component (`every_emitted_discharge_is_found_where_the_closure_claims_it`).
61. The retained adoption subjects are shapes rather than spends: fixture outpoints standing for no funded output, an empty witness at every input, and a fee paid in a foreign asset, which is what makes them decidable by the leaf's own bytes and is also why a target run of them observes a refusal before any compared field (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`).
62. The three facts that make the adoption gate's accepted case unreachable this wave are facts of the demonstration deployment, not of any run: a committed operator key of public, meaningless material whose scalar nobody holds, verified by the leaf's first instruction pair; a pinned singleton a node cannot issue, because a node derives asset identity from its own issuing outpoint; and a six-role witness whose successor program and nonce are exactly what the bundle's outstanding set says this link does not supply. Parameterizing the vectors' sources by singleton asset and committed operator key is the first bite that lifts them, and it belongs with the ABI side rather than with the closure tests (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`, `packages/linker/src/state_bundle.rs`).
63. The target's own words decide where each retained shape stops, and both rules are the target's rather than the run's: the three shapes that reach the leaf are refused `mandatory-script-verify-flag-failed (Invalid Schnorr signature)` on the script-path layer, and the short-output shape is refused `bad-txns-in-ne-out` on the consensus layer before any script runs, because an output of explicit value zero fails amount verification before any witness is examined. Since the leaf pins the amount at one, that case is node-unreachable by construction and a native reproduction of it needs a deployment whose pinned amount is above one. The target's relay policy admits no tapscript stack item wider than eighty bytes while the announcement's predecessor-metadata role declares eighty-six, so an announcement spend carrying real metadata is not relayable under default policy and is judged only at the block layer — a standing answer of the target that the ABI side inherits, whose available responses are restructuring the metadata, relaxing policy at deployment, or mining directly; the run capped that placeholder at the limit, which costs the observation nothing because no stack item's width reaches the first instruction pair (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`, `packages/realization/src/state_codec.rs`).
64. Two facts of the native adapter shape what a capture can be built on. It decides the observed layer from the mempool reason alone and returns that reason as the detail, so a case the mempool refuses by policy and the block refuses by script is reported as a consensus refusal carrying the policy reason while the block's script words survive only in the adapter's diagnostics — a capture of record built on the response alone would mis-file such a case. And it issues one asset per run by design, a second issuance spending the bare change coin while attaching the witness-form stack, so a run needing two assets takes the second through the sponsor path, which is the policy asset — the same asset the target weighs a fee in, which is what makes the accepted shape's fee at index two realizable in it.
65. No test or constant pins the composed leaf's instruction count or its encoded length. The checked totals compare script bytes against the encoder, the instruction count against the program itself and the validation budget against the contract's own per-opcode figures, which is the right shape for a total that must not drift from its source, but it leaves the instruction and byte figures the wave's bites reported regenerable by nothing, so they are not restated in this record (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_resource_tests.rs`).
66. The vectors' closure vocabulary has no closed-root walk. `MaturityClosureRefusal` holds 25 variants, of which six are reached by name in the closure tests and nineteen are neither reached nor declared unreachable with a reason, where the linker's own root is walked by an exhaustive match that fails to compile until every variant is accounted for. Giving this root the same walk is a first bite for whoever next touches the module (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`).
67. `T11-062` stays open at two located points: `ReferenceClass` in `packages/linker/src/graph.rs` carries no extension-point paragraph, and `MetadataAuthentication`'s paragraph cites the guide nowhere. Both are checkable in a diff, which is what the row asked for, and neither is a matter of judgement about the notes that did land.

### Wave-7 plan findings · `sec:phase6:wave7-findings`

The Wave-7 opening study was a read-only source study with no gate run, and its citations were self-audited; each was located again in the tree at the tip this record opens from before it was written here, and the totals below are the wave's baseline rather than a claim about the candidate the wave will build.

68. The conformance protocol carries seven operation subjects — funding, submission, sponsor funding, sponsor signing, script-path signing, confidential funding and confidential sponsor funding — matched by seven step kinds and by the capability each subject requires at schema 8, and the script-path signing subject under a committed public test signer handle already exists: the subject names the handle, the adapter advertises the capability from the machinery it actually has rather than by a hand-written claim, and the vectors' operator fixture produces the subject (`packages/target-elements-conformance/src/protocol.rs:229,605,2021-2075,2614-2658,2694`; `packages/vectors/src/maturity_operator.rs:354`; `scripts/elements-native-executor.py`). The wave's native exercise therefore needs no new protocol revision.
69. The native adapter's issued-asset state is keyed per asset — one retained reserve output per issued asset, keyed by the target's own spelling of it — and each issuance rebuilds the spendable change coin from the issuing transaction's own output, so a second issuance in one run is representable in the source; finding 64's one asset per run therefore reads as a property of the runs performed rather than as a limit of the adapter, and only a run settles it (`scripts/elements-native-executor.py:3587-3590,3853-3864`).
70. Three of the card's citations for ruling 40 and finding 41 had drifted by the time of this record and are corrected in place: the self-position pin's three instructions, the external evidence roles and the structural evidence census all moved when the reduction landed, and each is relocated here to the lines that now carry it (`packages/tapscript/src/state_pattern.rs:435-443,98-117,121-140`). The architecture citation beside them is exact, the PID asset still being declared with a fixed amount of one and not reissuable at the lines the card names (`packages/architecture/src/spec.rs:489-498`).
71. The target-generic signer capability at (`def:guide14-exec:signer-capability`) sketches a sixteen-bit input index and a single spent output, while the landed subject and the transaction-side signing types carry a thirty-two-bit index and one spent output per input (`packages/target-elements-conformance/src/protocol.rs:2286-2298`; `packages/transaction/src/live_census.rs:179,276,405`); and the typed request at (`def:guide14-exec:typed-request`) names a maturity requested-form type that exists nowhere in the tree, the inherited form and sponsor-change request types being the live generation's (`packages/transaction/src/live_request.rs:193-225`). Both are sketch-against-tree gaps that the ABI resolves in the tree's favour, which is what the guide's own minimization clause asks of that record.
72. The operator signing module states that metadata, successor-program, sponsor-input and fee-role changes after signing are decided by the exact echo and the recomputed message bindings and need no separate mutation variants, while (`rule:guide14-exec:signing-faults`) names those same four as required faults; the two statements are about different scopes, and ruling 57 settles it for the STATE chain (`packages/transaction/src/operator_signing.rs:425-429`).
73. Wave 7 carries a fifth exit obligation that its task text does not name: the register's determinism row `G14C-07` is owned by Waves 2 and 7 and stands OPEN, stating for this wave that a bound semantic execution plus the complete deployment, branch, funding, sponsorship, fee and signer-public environment determines exact candidate bytes; row `T11-074` closes it on equal complete input tuples giving equal candidate bytes, while the row's second demonstration — that distinct admitted sponsor environments need not share transaction bytes — belongs to the sponsored arm that ruling 58 defers; the four exits the wave's task at (`task:guide14-exec:wave7`) names do not include it (`plans/guides/guide_fourteen_conceptual_register.md`).
74. Exit 1 is blocked by a fact of the deployments rather than only by missing code: neither STATE deployment commits an operator key whose scalar a handle holds, both committing thirty-two-byte fills of public meaningless material, so no candidate over either can carry a signature that verifies however complete the ABI becomes (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs:291-335`).
75. No reviewed source carries the eighty-byte relay stack-item limit. It exists once in the tree, as a documented constant of a vectors test that caps the metadata placeholder at it, while the reviewed target's own relay vocabulary carries a fee-floor, a topology-restricted-package and a direct-submission condition and no width figure at all (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs:653,991`; `packages/target-elements/src/transaction_form.rs:232-247`). Ruling 53 moves it.
76. The transaction crate's refusal root holds 117 variants, counted mechanically by brace depth rather than read off a comment, so a STATE bite searches that vocabulary before minting a variant and the search is a real cost of every new refusal (`packages/transaction/src/error.rs:33-729`).
77. `SuccessorNonce`'s declared witness type is read from the copy-through component, while the carrier's witness attribution names the reconstruction component for the same role — deliberately, because the reconstruction authenticates the metadata whose representation that nonce fixes, which finding 54 states — so an ABI keying witness obligations by the schedule's source component rather than by the carrier's attribution would key that role to the wrong relation (`packages/tapscript/src/state_program.rs:315`; `packages/linker/src/state_carrier.rs:995-1008`).
78. The per-kind split of the 38 ABI obligations is derived from the derivation function and cross-checked against the asserted total rather than asserted anywhere: the outstanding-contract test asserts the total, that every obligation's relation appears on a row of the closure and that no obligation repeats, and says nothing about how the 38 divide by requirement kind (`packages/linker/src/state_carrier.rs:1051-1140`; `packages/linker/src/tests/state_carrier_tests.rs:824-845`).

The wave's figures are recounted here from the tree at the tip this record opens from, each from the test or constant that fixes it, rather than carried forward from the bites that reported them. Finding 76's baseline of 117 variants in the transaction crate's closed refusal root meets 139 at this tip, counted the same way and by brace depth rather than read off a comment (`packages/transaction/src/error.rs`), and the twenty-two the wave added belong to five of its nine code bites rather than to all of them: four to the public view, one to the ABI, six to construction, four to finalization and seven to signing. The typed request minted none, its one contradiction being refused by the live generation's own `SponsorChangeWithoutSponsoredForm`, which `a_sponsorless_request_asking_for_sponsor_change_is_refused` reads back; the relay figure minted none either, a reviewed-form verdict being a published fact and not a refusal. The whole root is still walked with no catch-all by `every_transaction_refusal_is_censused` (`packages/transaction/src/tests/refusal_census_tests.rs`), so each of the twenty-two had to be censused before the crate would compile again. The protected-region census is the twelve members of `MaturityProtectedRegion::ALL` in the order (`rule:guide14-exec:finalization`) states them, walked by `the_protected_region_census_is_the_guides_twelve_in_its_own_order` and exercised one region at a time by `every_protected_region_refuses_its_own_mutation_by_name` (`packages/transaction/src/tests/state_signing_tests.rs`). The settled-fact census is the eleven members of `MaturityFinalizedFact::ALL`, asserted whole by `the_settled_census_is_every_one_of_its_eleven_members` (`packages/transaction/src/tests/state_finalize_tests.rs`): the twelve regions less the executing leaf, whose fixing is the predecessor tree's rather than this transaction's, with the sponsor region and the fee role settled as absent rather than omitted. The witness schedule is seven records at widths one, four, eight, thirty-two, eighty-six, one and sixty-four in positions zero through six, published by the ABI and compared with the composed record's own schedule by `the_seven_records_are_the_schedules_own_roles_types_widths_and_positions` (`packages/transaction/src/tests/state_abi_tests.rs`), agreeing with the first-party declaration `witness_uses_component_types_in_declared_deepest_first_order` (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_program_tests.rs`) and read back off a built stack of nine — the seven items, then the leaf and the control block — by `the_chain_reaches_a_submit_ready_candidate_the_published_signer_authorized` (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_abi.rs`). The fifth of those widths is the canonical metadata encoding's `STATE_METADATA_BYTES` (`packages/realization/src/state_codec.rs`), eighty-six against the eighty of `TAPSCRIPT_STACK_ITEM_RELAY_LIMIT`, which is why ruling 53's verdict is a width and not a condition. The 38 outstanding ABI obligations are the linker's contract this wave answered against rather than a set the wave recounted into types of its own: `the_outstanding_contract_is_derived_keyed_and_equal_across_representations` still asserts 38, nineteen per representation and equal across the two (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_carrier_tests.rs`), and what the ABI answers is their witness half, one record per role whose consuming component is read from the carrier's own attribution rather than recomputed, which `every_records_consumer_is_the_carrier_maps_component_and_one_role_is_signer_held` checks role by role. The partition the ABI performs is over the link's five obligations and not over the 38: two discharged — the reconstruction half of `CurrentStateValidationUndischarged`, and `AbiUnsupplied` — and three carried, checked as two named sets against the link's own five with the halves disjoint by `the_partition_covers_the_links_five_exactly_with_disjoint_halves`, a named pair rather than one set computed as the other's complement, so an obligation a later wave adds lands in neither half and the derivation refuses. The ABI's own outstanding set is five members, the two residuals it mints beside one member per carried obligation, structurally non-empty and asserted in order by `the_outstanding_set_is_the_two_residuals_and_one_member_per_carried_obligation`. The deployment census is the three members of `MaturityDeployment::ALL`, the third committing the x-only key of the third published test signer handle beside a fixture singleton and a lead window of four and six (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`), and the golden is nine pinned constants of which the six the two earlier deployments fix are unmoved, all nine recomputed and compared with what each bundle carries in one comparison by `the_golden_roots_are_pinned_recomputed_and_carried_alike`. Three smaller censuses are recounted beside them, because each is a figure a later reader would otherwise re-derive: the public view's ten entries are seven stated and three projected (`the_entry_census_is_the_guides_ten_entries_in_its_order`, `the_three_projected_entries_are_read_from_the_entries_they_project`), the typed request's facets are three selectable and fifteen unselectable (`the_selectable_census_is_the_three_choices_the_guide_admits`, `the_unselectable_census_is_the_fifteen_choices_the_two_lists_refuse`), and the operator faults are the thirteen of (`rule:guide14-exec:signing-faults`) beside its four post-signing mutations, each reached by a test of its own.

Row `T11-070` is DONE on `0.6.206-dev`. Row `T11-072` is DONE on `0.6.208-dev`. Its gate asked that the finalized bytes survive the strict decoder's round trip; `the_finalized_bytes_survive_the_strict_decoders_round_trip` carries it, `the_output_census_is_the_successor_alone_with_no_sponsor_change_and_no_fee` and `the_settled_census_is_every_one_of_its_eleven_members` carry the finalized censuses, `the_spent_output_record_is_the_views_four_statements` holds the spent-output census that (`def:guide14-exec:signing-request`) binds to the view's own statements rather than to a restatement of them, the seven comparison tests from `the_offered_comparison_accepts_the_transaction_that_was_finalized` through `a_changed_input_sequence_is_refused_by_the_exact_byte_close` walk the exact-byte offered comparison in both directions, and `the_signing_request_derives_the_nine_census_terms_from_the_one_candidate`, `the_request_seed_is_the_bindings_genesis_in_the_messages_byte_order`, `the_requests_message_pair_names_two_distinct_candidates`, `the_request_publishes_no_opening_blinder_key_or_verdict` and `a_curve_refusing_the_committed_operator_key_refuses_the_signing_request` carry the signing request.

Row `T11-073` is DONE on `0.6.209-dev`. Its gate asked that every region's mutation after signing reject by name; `the_protected_region_census_is_the_guides_twelve_in_its_own_order` and `every_protected_region_refuses_its_own_mutation_by_name` walk the twelve, the thirteen operator faults of (`rule:guide14-exec:signing-faults`) are reached one test each from `a_missing_response_is_named` through `a_mismatched_leaf_selection_is_refused_by_the_freeze`, the four post-signing mutations are named by `a_changed_successor_metadata_is_named`, `a_changed_successor_program_is_named`, `a_sponsor_input_added_after_signing_is_named` and `a_fee_role_changed_after_signing_is_named` with `what_the_four_do_not_reach_falls_through_to_the_finalized_comparison` showing the exact-byte close catching what none of the four names, and `the_witness_is_the_records_seven_items_then_the_leaf_and_the_control_block`, `the_two_parity_bytes_are_the_two_constructors_and_not_the_view`, `a_refused_authorization_returns_its_token_for_the_reopened_state` and `the_submit_ready_bytes_decode_to_the_candidate_through_the_strict_decoder` carry the witness, the parities, the returned token and the candidate's own bytes.

Row `T11-074` is DONE on `0.6.210-dev`. Its gate asked that the wave's first exit be met and that `G14C-07` close only on the determinism property as a test; the nine tests of `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_abi.rs` carry it — `the_deployments_view_validates_through_the_real_curve_and_derives_its_abi` through the real curve rather than a fixture, `the_chain_reaches_a_submit_ready_candidate_the_published_signer_authorized` and `the_submit_ready_candidate_carries_the_finalized_bytes_unchanged` as the positive, `the_authorized_signature_verifies_against_the_committed_key_outside_the_chain` as the independent check, `a_signature_by_the_other_published_scalar_is_refused_for_the_frozen_message` and `a_response_naming_the_other_published_key_as_operator_is_refused` as the wrong-key control in both its faces, `the_finalized_and_candidate_bytes_decode_back_to_the_values_they_encode` as the strict-decoder round trip, and `equal_complete_input_tuples_give_equal_candidate_bytes` beside `a_different_admitted_cycle_gives_different_protected_bytes` as the determinism property and its one-term control. The row's conditional clause is not owed: the landing added a test file and its census line and touched no module carrying the vectors' refusal root, so the closed-root walk of finding 66 was not this row's to write and remains the named follow-on below.

Row `T11-075` is DONE on `0.6.211-dev`. Its gate asked that it close `T11-065`, which it does above; the gate this record runs is the documentation and label gate over the three plan files it edits, since a record that adds no code has no other evidence to offer, and every figure it carries is recounted from the tree at the tip it opens from rather than from the reports of the bites that produced them.

The wave's first exit — one sponsorless candidate becomes submit-ready — is MET on `0.6.210-dev`. The positive builds the whole chain over the third deployment and reaches a submit-ready candidate whose standing is the verifier's own, and the independent check re-freezes the request from the same finalized value, takes the committed key and the frozen message from that second derivation rather than from the state under test, and verifies the accepted authorization outside the chain that produced it; the two wrong-key faces refuse, one for a signature by another published scalar over the frozen message and one for a response naming another key as operator, and the registry records issue, signature and refusal as the events it is supposed to.

The second exit — sponsored forms become submit-ready where inherited policy claims them — is CONDITIONALLY MET, and the condition is named rather than argued around. Construction refuses the sponsored arm by a named typed refusal citing the absent carrier, `SponsoredMaturityFormHasNoCarrier`, which `a_sponsored_request_is_refused_for_the_carrier_it_would_need` reaches by name while the typed request keeps the form representable. Under ruling 40 the reduced leaf carries no sponsor check at all, and under ruling 52 the realization's sponsor relations are model-scope until the region-scoping refit of `T11-064` gives them a region, so inherited policy claims a sponsored STATE form only after that refit lands. The exit's own wording is therefore met by the refusal — where inherited policy claims nothing, there is nothing to make submit-ready — and the sponsored positive is that refit's to produce, not this wave's to fabricate.

The third exit — every protected mutation after signing rejects — is MET on `0.6.209-dev`, with the twelve regions refusing by the name of the region each reaches for and the four post-signing mutations of (`rule:guide14-exec:signing-faults`) named individually rather than left to the exact echo, which would decide them only as an undifferentiated wrong candidate.

The fourth exit — the production route exactly bound to finalization's leaf and protected bytes — is MET on `0.6.208-dev` and `0.6.209-dev`. All nine terms of (`rule:guide14-exec:census-binding`) derive from one finalized value, which `the_signing_request_derives_the_nine_census_terms_from_the_one_candidate` checks term by term against that candidate; authorization runs only through the construction right, the state's own entry calling the mediated route and nothing else, so no signature reaches a candidate without the registry observing it; and the executing leaf is the predecessor tree's committed subtree leaf rather than the link's relocated program, because the tapleaf hash the control block authenticates was taken over the committed bytes — `the_executing_leaf_is_the_committed_announcement_leaf_of_the_predecessors_tree` cross-reads it against the linked taptree's committed subtree instead of against the constructor that derived it, and `the_control_block_recomputes_to_the_predecessors_output_key_and_program` recomputes the control block to the predecessor's own output key.

`G14C-07` closes on the determinism property as a test and on nothing weaker. `equal_complete_input_tuples_give_equal_candidate_bytes` links two bundles independently, finalizes and signs each, and compares candidate bytes, protected bytes and witness stacks, so equality is between two builds rather than between one build and itself; `a_different_admitted_cycle_gives_different_protected_bytes` moves one term of the tuple inside the admitted window and shows the bytes move with it, which is what keeps the equality from being a statement about a constant. The row's second demonstration — that distinct admitted sponsor environments need not share transaction bytes — belongs to the sponsored arm ruling 58 defers, so the half that closed is stated as the half that closed (`0.6.210-dev`).

The Wave-7 clause of `G14C-03` is discharged on `0.6.207-dev` while the row itself stays OPEN for Wave 12. An exhausted successor search reaches the caller as the linker's own exhaustion refusal wrapped whole in one transaction variant rather than flattened into a generic construction failure, which `an_exhausted_search_carries_the_links_own_refusal` reads back and `exhaustion_a_wrong_nonce_and_a_later_admissible_nonce_are_three_findings` keeps distinct from the view's wrong-nonce finding and from a later-admissible one; a budget the host set running out is the host's policy and says nothing about what the target would accept, which is exactly the clause. Wave 12's half — the bounded corpus and attempts reported without promoting empirical success to totality — is untouched by this record.

What stays open is named here rather than left for a later study to rediscover. The relay restructure of ruling 53 is carried as the ABI's own `RelayAdmissibleWitnessSplitUnopened` rather than as prose, and it waits on whichever wave next opens the leaf, since splitting the eighty-six-byte item into relay-admissible pieces the leaf reassembles would move the witness schedule, its widths and order, the authentication's slicing, the leaf's bytes, the golden constants, the resource totals, the kept checks and the obligation contract. The freshness half of the split link obligation is carried as `CurrentStateRootFreshnessUnestablished` beside it, established by no layer in this chain — nothing in these crates observes a chain — and it waits on the branch-indexed history the register assigns to Wave 10 under `G14C-11`, which is where a current-root fact can come from without importing that machinery early. Finding 50's two `ScriptedCurve` stubs are still in the tree, one in the shared maturity fixtures and one local to the taptree tests, where one shared fixture would do (`packages/linker/src/tests/mod.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_taptree_tests.rs`). Finding 66's closed-root walk over `MaturityClosureRefusal` is still absent at twenty-five variants: the module was edited this wave, by the parameterization bite, which added a deployment and reused the existing `SourcesUnavailable` rather than minting a variant, so the root neither grew nor gained the walk, and giving it one stays a first bite for whichever wave next opens that file. And the three coordinator-rule types — `CoordinatorRule`, `LiveCoordinatorRule` and `MaturityCoordinatorRule` — each state one input index with the same derives and the same reader, which is a unification candidate for a generation-neutral ABI vocabulary rather than a defect in any of the three (`packages/transaction/src/abi.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/live_abi.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/state_abi.rs`). `T11-062` is not among these: its two points closed on `0.6.200-dev`, which gave `ReferenceClass` its extension-point paragraph and `MetadataAuthentication` its guide citation.

Wave 8 receives the native obligations at (`task:guide14-exec:wave8`). The exercise is ruling 61's fifth case, a submit-ready candidate read beside the four shapes the target refuses at the committed operator key, and that neighbourhood is what makes passing the fifth mean anything, because the four establish that the chain from linked bytes through static subtree, output key and control block is the one a target actually runs. It needs three things this wave supplies or fixes: a node-issued singleton, which the parameterized sources of ruling 54 now accept because a deployment's three values are supplied rather than written in; the signer of ruling 54, whose scalar a published handle holds and whose x-only key the third deployment commits; and the relay verdict of ruling 53, because the executor asks the mempool first and offers a transaction it refuses for something other than a script verdict to `generateblock`, reporting `relay_policy_rejection` when that block accepts: an announcement spend meets a standardness width measured before execution, so it is mined and reported as a relay-policy refusal, and finding 64 at (`sec:phase6:wave6-findings`) states the narrower case in which the block also refuses (`scripts/elements-native-executor.py:4584-4664`). What the node-free positive established is that the ABI can reach a submit-ready candidate whose signature verifies against the key a deployment committed, and that every protected region and every operator fault behaves as the guide requires; what only the run can establish is that a target's own interpreter accepts those bytes, and what this record claims for the positive is exactly what the tree carries — no submission, no node, in-process verification only, with the standing recorded as `InProcessVerified` naming its verifier and nothing stronger.

79. The predecessor output-key prefix byte is the parity of the commitment that reconstructs the predecessor program, and it is recoverable from neither the public view nor the program itself: the view states the predecessor program as the target does, an x-only key with no prefix in it and no parity beside it. The ABI names the commitment as that role's source rather than the view, and the witness population reads the byte from the retained predecessor constructor while the successor's comes from the successor constructor, two different constructors, so taking either from the other would state one point's parity about the other (`packages/transaction/src/state_abi.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/state_signing.rs`).
80. The refusal root's `Clone + PartialEq + Eq` derives cannot carry the affine construction-right token, so a refused STATE authorization returns the token beside the refusal in a `Debug`-only failure type mirroring the registry's own `RightFailure`. The shape is load-bearing rather than incidental: a refusal grants no new authority and takes none away, the refused scope stays outstanding under a recorded refusal event precisely so it can be consumed again, and a signature that dropped the token would let one malformed operator response lock a scope for good (`packages/transaction/src/state_signing.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/operator_right.rs`, `a_refused_authorization_returns_its_token_for_the_reopened_state`).
81. The adoption vectors lay out no STATE witness — every input witness in those transactions is empty — so no file in the tree assembled the seven-item stack before the signing states landed. The order and the widths are declared first-party by the composed program's own schedule and republished by the ABI's records, which is why both had to be tested against that schedule rather than against a vector (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`, `packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_program_tests.rs`).
82. Several refusals in the wave's modules are argued unreachable in place rather than reached, and the arguments are of different kinds, which is worth recording because only one of them is an argument this crate makes about its own code. No witness-width refusal was minted at all: each of the seven items is fixed-width by the encoding it is read from, so a refusal for their disagreement could never be reached and the agreement is held by tests against each record's declared minimum and maximum instead. `OperatorRightOutcome::Cached` is unreachable through the production route, the registry's consumption always invoking the signer, and its arm is written to be right rather than to be reached. The non-prototype refusal rests on a fact in another crate, the linked bundle's status being a `const fn` with no setter behind it, and the view's and the ABI's candidate and revision refusals are raised by both entries and reached by no test in either file for that reason. The continuity refusal is the exception that is reached: `a_constructor_over_another_static_subtree_fails_the_continuity_equality` reaches it through the helper the entry calls, so only the path through the entry is unexercised. And `WrongLeaf` is a freeze-time fault the chain cannot reach with a crafted response, since the executing leaf is derived inside the request builder with no parameter through which another could arrive, so it is reached at the freeze instead and the chain's answer for a caller reaching elsewhere is the executing-leaf region's own refusal.
83. Clippy's `missing_const_for_fn` is live for this workspace: the nursery group is set to warn and warnings are denied, and three packages carry an explicit allow for it by name, which is what a lint nobody has to satisfy does not need (`Cargo.toml`, `packages/architecture/src/lib.rs`, `packages/model/src/lib.rs`, `packages/realization/src/lib.rs`). The consequence is visible in the signing states: all twelve region mutators are `const fn` because each body builds one refusal from a `Copy` region and nothing else, while a method that reads through a borrow or expects a value is not.
84. (`def:guide14-exec:state-view`) names the current cycle twice, its seventh entry being a field of the predecessor metadata the third entry already states, which is why three of the view's ten entries are projections read through their sources rather than stated: the cycle through the metadata, and the operator identity and the deployment symbols through the accepted bundle. A separate statement of any of the three would put one fact in two places with nothing in the view able to settle a disagreement, so the no-overwrite law is structural for those three and a refusal for the seven that are stated (`packages/transaction/src/state_view.rs`).
85. The transaction crate's README now mixes two layouts: its introduction and its four pre-Wave-7 sections are hard-wrapped at seventy-one to seventy-nine columns, while the wave's six sections are one line per paragraph, from 914 to 3437 characters. The wave's sections are the ones that match how this repository's prose is formatted; the older ones are legacy, and the file reads as two files until somebody reflows them (`packages/transaction/README.md`).
86. The exhaustion fixture's scanned figure is asserted rather than assumed. The scan finds the least quantity whose predecessor commits at nonce zero and whose successor does not, and the test asserts the figure it settles on is fifteen, because a figure nobody checks can drift with the fixture curve, the composed record or the metadata codec without anybody noticing that the test stopped exercising exhaustion at all (`packages/transaction/src/tests/state_construct_tests.rs`).
87. The finalization's deployment seed is read in the message's byte order, which is the reverse of the binding's genesis, and the test observes it against a deliberately non-palindromic identity beside the demonstration one, so a reversed genesis is a different deployment rather than the same one read twice (`the_request_seed_is_the_bindings_genesis_in_the_messages_byte_order`).
88. The executing leaf is the predecessor tree's committed subtree leaf and not the link's relocated program, because the tapleaf hash the control block authenticates was taken over the committed bytes. The test cross-reads it through the accepted bundle's taptree rather than through the constructor that derived the recipe, so two accounts of one tree are compared rather than assumed equal, and it checks the tree's own merkle root against the predecessor's beside them (`packages/transaction/src/tests/state_finalize_tests.rs`).
89. The refusal root grew by twenty-two variants across the wave, and the growth is not evenly spread: the public view four, the ABI one, construction six, finalization four and signing seven, with the typed request and the relay figure minting none. Each of the twenty-two is reached by name or argued unreachable in place, and each had to be censused in the crate's exhaustiveness guard before the crate would compile again, so the cost of a new refusal is paid at the moment it is minted (`packages/transaction/src/error.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/tests/refusal_census_tests.rs`).
90. The finalized output census fixes the fee role absent and the sponsor-change position absent, because the singleton amount conserves across the one output and the announcement form has no sponsor region to spend from. Absent is a settled state and not a vacancy, which is why an output beyond the census after signing is refused as the fee role changing at that position, and a second input as a sponsor input added, rather than as a slot being filled (`packages/transaction/src/state_finalize.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/state_signing.rs`).
91. The eighty-byte relay limit now has one definition, in the reviewed target beside the relay conditions, with its policy source named by file path and its kind argued — standardness and not consensus, which is why it carries a figure where the relay floor deliberately carries none. Finding 75's citation of the vectors test's constant is therefore historical: that test reads the target's figure, and the target's own tests pin the value (`packages/target-elements/src/transaction_form.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`).
92. A tapscript policy test counted the reviewed transaction forms as a hard-coded pair and had to move when the announcement form joined the census, which made every future reviewed form a failure of a test that is not about reviewed forms. The repair compares the projection's keys against the target's own list instead of against a written-down length, keeping the explicit sponsorship checks that test actually owns, so the blind spot closes rather than moving one form along (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/policy_tests.rs`, `0.6.204-dev`).
93. No real-arithmetic curve capability was reachable from the transaction crate's tests before the view bite, and none is now: the crate is forbidden `vectors` outright, and its `FixtureStateCurve` and `FixtureLiveCurve` answer the two vocabularies from one shared tweak whose last byte decides the parity, which is what makes the parity structurally observable in a fixture at all. The real curve is the vectors' own oracle pair, which is why the end-to-end positive lives there and validates its view through real arithmetic (`packages/transaction/src/tests/state_support.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/live_capability.rs`).
94. Finding 66's closed-root walk over `MaturityClosureRefusal` stays open, and the reason is narrower than the wave leaving the file alone. The parameterization bite edited that module, adding a third deployment whose published key can be unavailable, and answered that case with the existing `SourcesUnavailable` rather than minting a variant, so the root is still twenty-five variants with no exhaustive match behind it. The metadata, nonce and program the node-free positive needed were reachable through the bundle's retained constructor application, so nothing else in the wave had to open the file either (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`).
95. Two figures in the wave's own test prose drifted as the root grew, and neither is load-bearing, which is exactly why nothing caught them: the exhaustiveness guard's paragraph reasons about "a hundred and six identifiers" where its two matches now list 139 and 39, and construction's per-module census reasons about "a hundred and twenty-eight variants" where the root held 128 at that landing and holds 139 now. Both are prose about a count rather than a count anything asserts, so the next transaction bite that edits either file corrects them in passing (`packages/transaction/src/tests/refusal_census_tests.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/tests/state_construct_tests.rs`).

### Wave-8 plan findings · `sec:phase6:wave8-findings`

The Wave-8 opening study was a read-only source study with no gate run, and its citations were self-audited; each was located again in the tree at the tip this record opens from before it was written here, three of its figures did not survive that relocation and stand below as the recount rather than as the number they were carried in, and the totals are the wave's baseline rather than a claim about the evidence the wave will build.

96. The Wave-7 closure record's handoff sentence misapplied finding 64, and this record corrects it in place. The adapter does not decide the observed layer from the mempool reason alone: a mempool refusal naming no script verdict is offered to `generateblock`, and the observation is `relay_policy_rejection` when that block accepts and a consensus refusal before script when it does not, the mempool's reason deciding in the second case because block validation cannot tell a conservation failure from a script failure (`scripts/elements-native-executor.py:4584-4664,4666-4722`). An announcement spend meets a standardness width measured before execution, so the block accepts it: the spend is mined, and it is reported as a relay-policy refusal rather than as a consensus one. Finding 64 at (`sec:phase6:wave6-findings`) is correct about the case it names — one the mempool refuses by policy and the block refuses by script — and the drift was carrying that case's conclusion to a case with the other outcome.
