The same clause also stands inside ruling 61, where it is a reason offered for a settled ruling rather than a statement about the adapter; ruling 64 replaces its factual premise, the holding it supports — that the native exercise extends the four-shape carrier with a fifth case rather than replacing it — does not turn on it, and this record edits no landed ruling's text.

97. Ruling 14's citation of the backlog's §3.4 does not resolve, and this record corrects it in place. §3.4 is the readiness statement (`rem:backlog:readiness`); the dependency-admission rule is §8.2 at (`rule:backlog:dependency-entry`), whose eight conditions ask for a concrete consumer, a demonstrated gap, the insufficiency of simpler exact first-party code, a review of source, version, licence, MSRV, unsafe boundary, transitive graph, determinism and advisories, a leakage judgement, focused tests with an independent oracle, a lockfile review, and green gates. Every one of those conditions is shaped for a third-party crate, and the table the rule governs lists third-party crates only. Whether a first-party workspace crate entering another crate's dependency list needs the record is stated nowhere: the rule's single mention of first-party code is the condition that simpler first-party code be insufficient, which is about the alternative to a dependency rather than about an edge inside the workspace.

98. `packages/vectors/src/maturity_operator.rs` is the live-transfer operator ceremony and not the four-shape observation carrier. Its own opening line states what it is — one synthetic operator authorization over public live-transfer fixtures — it builds through the live representation plan and the live finalization, and it carries eleven native steps and eleven evidence rows (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_operator.rs:1-8,52-79`). The four-shape run belongs to the adoption vectors: a node-gated test over `AdoptionCase` that compares each shape's observed layer against the refusal its own construction predicts and writes a capture, landed by `0.6.198-dev` (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs:1386-1429`). Ruling 61 names that carrier by what it does rather than by a file, so the ruling is unaffected and only the attribution needed fixing.

99. §16 recounts to 206 rows at this tip — 14, 11, 16, 18, 15, 15, 14, 16, 15, 14, 23, 22 and 13 across the thirteen tables, 16.3 counted as eleven listed faults plus one row per unaffected semantic field. The guide assigns deliverables to waves and never rows: the Wave-8 task lists nine deliverables and four exits and names no table (`plans/guides/guide_fourteen.md:3070-3096`), so every row-to-wave mapping this record makes is an inference stated as one rather than a reading of the guide.

100. §16's preamble names nine per-row facts, and the live row carries five of them. `LiveSafetyRow` has seven fields: a section and a name, which are its identity, and then polarity, mutation layer, declared boundary, intended relation and collateral closure (`packages/vectors/src/live_safety.rs:363-432`). The intended carrier, the canonical control, the mutation locator and the expected projection have no field on it; the control identity exists only on an observed standing, where a run computed it (`packages/vectors/src/live_evidence.rs:687-722`). A STATE row carrying all nine is therefore wider than the live row by four facts, which is the recount the opening study's figure of seven did not survive — seven is the field count and not the overlap.

101. §14.2's eight refusal fields split four and four against the live standing. Declared boundary, observed layer, accepted control identity and refusal detail are the four fields of `NativeRefusalObserved`; the submitted subject identity or exact bytes, the mutation locator or structural separator, the intended carrier, and whether the carrier executed have no field anywhere in that standing, against the eight the evidence standing at (`rule:guide14-exec:standing`) requires a native refusal to store (`packages/vectors/src/live_evidence.rs:687-722`).

102. §14.1's eight plan inputs split five and three at this tip. The validated compiler operation plan, the exact linked bundle, the exact candidate ABI, the exact target and deployment binding and the expected executor provenance all exist; the canonical semantic fixture registry, the canonical constructor mutation registry and the canonical root-history mutation registry do not exist for STATE in any form, and no module of the evidence crate declares one (`packages/vectors/src/lib.rs:4-64`).

103. The agreement of expected and observed layers is already enforced at both sites that mint a native refusal standing, so what this wave adds is a standing and not a rule. The maturity-operator ceremony mints `NativeRefusalObserved` only where the row's expected layer, declared per subject before anything executes, equals the observed one (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_operator.rs:226-235,263-284`). The corpus retyping refuses with `RefusalBoundaryMismatch` unless both the row's own refusing layer and `observed_boundary` of the observed layer equal the declared boundary (`packages/vectors/src/live_evidence.rs:1941-1955`). `observed_boundary` maps both non-verdict layers to `None`, including the infrastructure member of the same name, and `ObservedOutcomeLayer::is_target_verdict` separates them at the source (`packages/vectors/src/observed_boundary.rs:41-99`; `packages/target-elements-conformance/src/protocol.rs:1375-1392`). What is absent is a STATE standing: the unexpected-boundary and infrastructure-blocked members, their exclusion from an answered standing, and the census buckets that count them exist for the live rows alone (`packages/vectors/src/live_evidence.rs:723,859,933-969,1977-1994`).

104. The schema-8 submission subject carries transaction bytes and nothing else, and its own record states why: no expected layer, no expected identity and no class, because under the sole current revision the answer stays with the caller (`packages/target-elements-conformance/src/protocol.rs:229,2160-2172`). `validate_observation_for_kind`'s `Submit` arm requires an accepted identity and a mined readback on `accepted` and forbids either on anything else, so the identity `generateblock` computes for a relay-refused spend cannot be reported at all today (`packages/target-elements-conformance/src/protocol.rs:3215-3226`). The case workload is the contrast that makes this a protocol fact rather than an oversight: there a fixture declares its layer and is judged in the matching engine, and the declaration exists because a policy rule refusing without running the script had looked like a harness failure (`scripts/elements-native-executor.py:3181-3201`).

105. The node's argument list carries no standardness relaxation. It sets a zero relay-fee floor and a zero block-minimum fee, disables pegin validation with its reason cited, and names no policy-widening flag (`scripts/elements-native-executor.py:2182-2204`); a search of the packages, scripts and plans finds no artifact recording an attempt to relax one.

106. `PublicTestSignerHandle` has two members, `First` and `Third`, resolving to the first and third published scalars, while `SECOND_SCALAR` is published beside them with no handle that reaches it (`packages/target-elements-conformance/src/test_material.rs:100,124-153`).

107. The compact fixture registry takes the realization path alone and records that it does: its successor amount is derived with the realization layer's own checked arithmetic and never by asking the constructor what it settled on (`packages/vectors/src/fixture.rs:11-21`). The typed projection adapter of (`rule:guide14-exec:expected-semantics`) exists only as private functions of a test module the model crate compiles under `cfg(test)` (`packages/model/src/lib.rs:62-63`; `packages/model/src/tests/state_metadata_conformance_tests.rs:32,58`), and the evidence crate does not depend on the model crate, its manifest declining the edge deliberately for want of a call (`packages/vectors/Cargo.toml:29-33,115-123`) while the package contract at (`pkg:vectors:contract`) names that crate among the package's expected direct dependencies.

108. The evidence crate declares 61 modules at this tip: two STATE ones, the closure sources and the operator ceremony, beside 35 of the live generation's and 24 compact and shared ones (`packages/vectors/src/lib.rs:4-64`). No STATE fixture, matrix, evidence, first-party, report, native or corpus module exists, so every module the rows below name is a new file rather than an extension of one. The recount also corrects the opening study's figure: the non-STATE modules number fifty-nine and not forty-two.

The wave's figures are recounted from the tree this record opens from rather than from earlier gate reports. Finding 99's denominator remains 206: `MATURITY_SAFETY_ROWS` and `the_matrix_transcribes_every_table_at_its_recounted_width` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs` give thirteen widths of 14, 11, 16, 18, 15, 15, 14, 16, 15, 14, 23, 22 and 13, matching §16's lists with §16.3's eleven faults expanded by the five unaffected fields of `StateMetadata` in `packages/realization/src/state.rs`. The evidence partition is 40 first-party discharged, 43 first-party required, 42 native-run required, 15 report-layer required, 65 typed non-answers and one native declared-boundary observation; native mutant refusals and native acceptances both count zero, 41 rows are answered, 165 remain outstanding, and `every_required_row_is_answered()` is false (`the_census_figures_are_unmoved_by_the_binding_readers` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`; `the_completeness_is_partial_at_this_tip` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`). The 83 pre-target cases divide into 40 discharges and 43 carried cases, with 14 validators and an empty deferred-owner set (`the_census_covers_the_pre_target_rows_exactly_and_nothing_else`, `the_two_dispositions_partition_the_census`, `every_discharge_meets_the_policy_against_its_own_control` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`); `MaturityCarriedReason` has eleven members but only ten populated reasons, because deferral is retained without a case. The negative-half register is narrower than all outstanding work: `STILL_REQUIRED` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_negative_half.rs` lists the 42 native-run-required rows, whose gaps recount to 19 mutation-staging, five missing-shape, nine program-derivation, two program-generic-refusal and seven unpublished-relation entries, with the sixth vocabulary member, `TargetRunNotYetPlanned`, empty after admission; `every_outstanding_row_is_registered`, `the_gap_census_accounts_for_every_row` and `the_register_length_is_the_figure_the_plan_recomputes` hold that list against the classifier. The fourteen positive rows are independently keyed by `PositiveRow::ALL` and `the_census_answers_every_positive_row_exactly_once` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs`; the 24 locator members map totally to 24 subject members, checked by `every_locator_names_one_subject_and_every_subject_stands_on_a_row` in the matrix. The standing enum now has fifteen members; the module-local refusal roots count 25 in `maturity_closure.rs`, nine in `maturity_first_party.rs`, fourteen in `maturity_evidence.rs`, twenty in `maturity_report.rs`, seventeen in `maturity_native.rs` and twelve in `maturity_corpus.rs`, while the fixture, matrix and negative-half register add no refusal root. The safety report has thirteen private fields, twelve recomputed items, eleven volatile-field exclusions and three completeness states, with `PartialRequiredRowsOutstanding` at this tip (`MaturityAnnouncementSafetyReport`, `MaturityRecomputedItem::ALL`, `MaturityVolatileField::ALL`, `MaturitySafetyCompleteness`, `the_report_assembles_and_validates_on_the_derived_plan`, `the_exclusions_are_the_guides_eleven_and_none_is_a_rendered_key`). `ANNOUNCEMENT_STEPS` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs` has three steps, its run evidence two standings and two acceptance routes; the committed `RUN-REPORT` under `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/` records one test, one ceremony and zero setups, `native-maturity-run-report 1` and `native-maturity-capture 1`, while the capture itself carries `native-capture-schema 2`, protocol schema 8 and an embedded `maturity-evidence-schema 1`. Its adapter is `elements-native-executor` version `2.1.0`, its node is Elements Core daemon version `v28.99.0` with the recorded build suffix retained by the validated reader, and its sponsorless response is `relay_policy_rejection` with `bad-witness-nonstandard`; `the_admitted_bytes_derive_the_boundary_and_leave_acceptance_outstanding` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs` checks those bindings without turning the observation into acceptance. Finding 108's 61 module declarations have become 69 in `packages/vectors/src/lib.rs`, with the eight new STATE evidence modules present. Their source `#[test]` attributes recount to 17 in `maturity_fixture.rs`, 18 in `maturity_safety.rs`, 19 in `maturity_first_party.rs`, 16 in `maturity_evidence.rs`, seven in `maturity_negative_half.rs`, sixteen in `maturity_report.rs`, 21 in `maturity_native.rs` and nineteen in `maturity_corpus.rs`; the closure source adds one, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs` has 27 including one ignored native test, and `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_native.rs` has seven including one ignored native test. These are source inventories, not claimed test-run totals.

Row `T11-076` is DONE on `0.6.212-dev`. Its gate asked for the documentation and label gate over the opening record's three plan files; the eight rulings at (`rule:phase6:wave8-rulings`), the opening findings at (`sec:phase6:wave8-findings`), the fifteen backlog rows and the register's Wave-8 paragraph are present, and this record closes the opening row on the documentation and label gate rather than claiming another source test for prose.

Row `T11-077` is DONE on `0.6.213-dev`. Its gate asked that another refusal variant stop compilation until censused; `every_closure_refusal_is_censused` is an exhaustive match without a wildcard and `the_whole_closure_refusal_root_is_censused` names all 25 variants in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`. The match lives inside the declaring crate because the root is non-exhaustive outside it; its arms identify fifteen reached refusals and ten unreachable guards, while the supporting calls, including `a_zero_lead_minimum_leaves_the_sources_unavailable` and `two_leaves_of_different_shapes_are_not_comparable`, live in `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`.

Row `T11-078` is DONE on `0.6.214-dev`. Its gate asked for a semantic registry carrying no target program, control block, transaction bytes or signature; `a_case_renders_no_target_vocabulary` checks that separation, `each_successor_is_the_realization_transitions_own_answer` and `the_successor_differs_from_its_predecessor_in_maturity_alone` bind the expected successor to the semantic transition, and `the_outstanding_members_are_present_with_their_reasons` keeps the executable-model and typed-projection producers explicit (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs`).

Row `T11-079` is DONE on `0.6.215-dev`. Its gate asked for 206 rows and an asserted count for every section; `the_matrix_transcribes_every_table_at_its_recounted_width` supplies both, `every_row_is_named_once` checks identity by section and name, and `each_typed_non_answer_stands_on_exactly_the_rows_its_reason_names` holds every non-answer to its own population (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`).

Row `T11-080` is DONE on `0.6.217-dev`. Its gate asked that every linkable row resolve and every unlinked row state why; `every_declared_row_resolves_in_the_sponsorless_case_of_every_representation`, `every_resolved_requirement_is_the_negative_the_row_named` and `two_rows_share_a_requirement_only_by_sharing_their_declaration` check the 101 active resolutions in each representation against the plan, while `the_whole_transaction_scoped_rows_are_exactly_the_recounted_set` keeps the seventeen whole-transaction rows unlinked for their stated reason (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`). A requirement link is not a discharge at the row's evidence boundary.

Row `T11-081` is DONE on `0.6.216-dev`. Its gate asked that each in-wave class predicate run against the registry; `each_in_wave_class_holds_on_its_own_row_and_the_lead_classes_reject_each_other` checks the semantic predicates, `the_nonce_class_reads_the_links_own_retained_search` checks the search subject independently of a semantic fixture, `the_out_of_wave_classes_answer_exactly_the_sponsored_and_recovery_rows` keeps the other classes explicit, and `the_registry_reads_its_row_names_from_the_safety_matrix` prevents a second name table (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs`).

Row `T11-082` is DONE on `0.6.218-dev`, `0.6.219-dev` and `0.6.222-dev`. Its gate asked for a case for every pre-target row and no discharge without its honest control; `the_census_covers_the_pre_target_rows_exactly_and_nothing_else` establishes total classification, `the_two_dispositions_partition_the_census` holds forty discharges beside forty-three typed carried cases with no deferred owner, and `every_discharge_meets_the_policy_against_its_own_control` runs each owning validator twice. `a_validator_that_is_not_the_rows_owner_does_not_discharge_it`, `the_link_does_not_discharge_a_linked_program_row` and `the_operation_response_check_does_not_discharge_a_metadata_row` prevent discharging by a nearby refusal (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

Row `T11-083` is DONE on `0.6.220-dev`. Its gate asked that no ordinary standing accept the class-3 marker by type; `MaturityRowStanding` has no marker-bearing member or conversion, `a_poison_marker_carries_its_bytes_and_no_standing_can_hold_it` retains the model-falsifying observation's verbatim bytes outside that vocabulary, and `the_census_renders_the_quantifier_the_guarantees_are_stated_under` states class 1 and class 2 conditional on no class 3. `the_plan_exposes_exactly_the_eight_inputs` and `both_outstanding_registries_carry_their_reason_and_count_zero` prevent omitted inputs from looking like fulfilled ones (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`).

Row `T11-084` is DONE on `0.6.221-dev`. Its gate asked that register and census agree both ways; `every_outstanding_row_is_registered` compares both set differences, `the_register_holds_no_duplicate` checks multiplicity, and `the_gap_census_accounts_for_every_row` and `the_register_length_is_the_figure_the_plan_recomputes` hold the current 42 entries against `NativeRunRequired` after the sponsorless row's admission (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_negative_half.rs`).

Row `T11-085` is DONE on `0.6.223-dev`. Its gate asked for equal canonical bytes from two derivations of one plan; `two_derivations_of_one_plan_render_identical_canonical_bytes` performs those independent derivations, `the_canonical_bytes_do_not_move_with_the_timing` keeps timing separate, and `the_validation_refuses_an_altered_answered_figure_naming_its_item`, `the_validation_refuses_an_altered_completeness_token` and `the_validation_refuses_a_truncated_row_block` refuse edited summaries. `the_witness_stands_on_the_discharged_rows_and_names_what_it_is_owed` witnesses the soundness clause without claiming all rows are covered (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`).

Row `T11-086` is DONE on `0.6.224-dev`. Its gate asked for the fourth exit's exact-subject binding; the matrix's four sweep tests are `every_locator_names_one_subject_and_every_subject_stands_on_a_row`, `the_rows_that_point_nowhere_are_exactly_the_positive_fourteen`, `each_control_shape_stands_only_on_rows_whose_subject_it_can_build` and `the_observed_product_reaches_each_row_at_its_own_boundary_only`. The evidence sweep's six are `a_refusal_matching_every_fact_binds_and_a_departed_boundary_or_subject_does_not`, `a_departed_carrier_or_control_does_not_bind`, `a_structural_separator_answers_only_the_subject_that_sits_between_fields`, `no_refusal_binds_to_a_row_that_admits_none`, `from_native_refusal_classifies_every_declared_row_at_every_observed_layer` and `the_census_figures_are_unmoved_by_the_binding_readers` (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`). The exit remains conditional on the sponsor-envelope incidence named below rather than silently retyping that row.

Row `T11-087` is DONE on `0.6.225-dev`. Its gate asked that the planner's declared layers match the matrix; `declared_layers_match_the_matrix_and_operator_infrastructure` compares the submission and both funding steps, `scripted_accepted_responses_follow_the_exact_sequence` checks the three-step order, and `submission_is_independently_rebuilt_over_the_reported_outpoint` checks the exact candidate. The current `the_register_no_longer_requires_the_sponsorless_target_run` pins the admitted observation after `0.6.227-dev`; retaining its former requires-the-run assertion would contradict the register it reads (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`).

Row `T11-088` is DONE on `0.6.226-dev`, with its capture committed by `0.6.227-dev`. Its gate asked for the row's declared relay observation while acceptance stayed outstanding; `the_maturity_announcement_runs_against_a_real_target` and the capture driver are present in `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_native.rs` and `scripts/live-native-maturity-capture.sh`, and the run of record is the committed capture under `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/` that the admission pins, not a commit treated as an observation. `evidence_retains_the_declared_refusal_and_outstanding_acceptance` and `accepted_observation_is_off_declaration_and_acceptance_stays_outstanding` distinguish the two standings in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`; `payload_retains_standing_context_and_both_outstanding_acceptance_routes` and `scripted_capture_retains_replay_operands_before_gates` preserve that distinction in the integration file's capture contract.

Row `T11-089` is DONE on `0.6.227-dev`. Its gate asked for strict admission of the committed capture and refusal of a moved byte; `the_admitted_bytes_derive_the_boundary_and_leave_acceptance_outstanding`, `every_file_byte_flip_stops_at_its_outer_layer`, `census_and_every_file_length_are_fixed`, `outer_rebinding_does_not_replace_the_capture_content_hash` and `admission_passes_recorded_request_changes_to_exact_replay` establish the layered admission in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`. Its fixed four-file archive is `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`; the evidence classifier answers the sponsorless row as `NativeDeclaredBoundaryObserved`, while `the_positive_boundary_names_the_admitted_run_without_claiming_acceptance` keeps both native-acceptance and mutant-refusal counts at zero (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`).

Row `T11-090` is DONE on the label gate, this record. Its gate asked that it close `T11-076`; the documentation and label gate over these three plan files checks the closure record, and the recount, dispositions and handoffs above and below give the opening row an explicit outcome without assigning this record its own patch tag.

The first exit at (`task:guide14-exec:wave8`) — every safety row answered or explicitly outstanding — is MET. `the_plan_classifies_every_row_of_the_matrix_exactly_once` and `required_rows_remain_outstanding_after_the_admitted_run` partition all 206 rows; the 42 run-waiting rows each have a typed gap in `STILL_REQUIRED`, the 43 first-party-required rows retain their carried reasons, the fifteen report-layer-required rows retain their expected projections, and the 65 non-answers retain the matrix's typed reasons (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_negative_half.rs`). Explicit coverage of the outstanding set is the exit; the report's 165 outstanding rows forbid reading it as complete evidence.

The second exit — no wrong-boundary refusal counted as answered — is MET. `only_the_discharged_and_observed_standings_are_answered` excludes `NativeRefusalAtUnexpectedBoundary`, and `from_native_refusal_classifies_every_declared_row_at_every_observed_layer` walks 987 row/layer pairs, with 43 boundary matches but only 42 mutant bindings because the positive sponsorless row is its own control. `native_refusal_binds_to_row` additionally checks the row's boundary, subject, control shape and executed carrier, so equality to a self-declared boundary alone does not bind another row (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`; §1.15 at (`rule:guide14-exec:boundary-equality`)).

The third exit — no infrastructure result treated as a target verdict — is MET. `declared_layers_match_the_matrix_and_operator_infrastructure` gives both funding steps no declared row layer, `each_funding_step_refuses_every_nonaccepted_layer` requires their acceptance before construction proceeds, and `only_the_discharged_and_observed_standings_are_answered` excludes `InfrastructureBlocked` (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`). Funding can therefore establish a coin without answering a safety row, and a failed environment remains its own standing.

The fourth exit — controls and mutants exact-subject bound — is CONDITIONALLY MET. The sweep named in `T11-086` establishes the total locator-to-subject map and compares boundary, control shape and executed carrier over the full declared-row/layer product; the incidence table preserves one internal inconsistency, §16.11's `state-and-sponsor-positions-exchanged`, which changes `SponsorEnvelope` while departing from `SponsorlessAnnouncement`, a control with no envelope to exchange (`CONTROL_AGAINST_SUBJECT`, `each_control_shape_stands_only_on_rows_whose_subject_it_can_build` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`). Correcting that row's subject/control contract is the condition, and a transcription test cannot make it true by recounting it. The binding predicate settles the row-side facts it can read; actual control acceptance and equality to the construction's submitted bytes remain run-side evidence, as `native_refusal_binds_to_row` states, and zero observed native mutant refusals are promoted by the sweep (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`).

The Wave-8 half of `G14C-12` is discharged on `0.6.220-dev`, while the row remains OPEN. The closed three-class vocabulary, the separate branch-poison marker carrying verbatim evidence, the exclusion of that marker from every ordinary STATE standing, the class-1/class-2 and no-class-3 quantifier, and the class-3 test are carried by `a_poison_marker_carries_its_bytes_and_no_standing_can_hold_it` and `the_census_renders_the_quantifier_the_guarantees_are_stated_under` (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`). Wave 10 still owes typed class-2 responses and the competing-spend, same-key-instance and reorganization-surfaced-realization tests; those require the branch context its history and recovery work supplies, so a poison-marker test closes none of them.

The Wave-8 report clause of `G14C-02` is witnessed on `0.6.223-dev`, while the row remains OPEN. `MaturityRefinementClause::SoundnessOfRefusedSteps` states that refused concrete steps admit no allowed semantic step at their declared boundary, and `the_witness_stands_on_the_discharged_rows_and_names_what_it_is_owed` limits that witness to the forty first-party discharges, with no native mutant refusal and 165 rows still owed (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`). The forward-simulation clause still requires an accepted concrete step and Wave 9's predecessor and successor projections; the typed abstraction relation and supported-step completeness under named representability assumptions remain with the other owning Waves 2, 9, 10 and 13 (`plans/guides/guide_fourteen_conceptual_register.md`). A partial soundness witness does not supply those statements by implication.

The seventh conjunct at (`gate:guide14-exec:exit`) is OUTSTANDING. The committed sponsorless capture reports `relay_policy_rejection` with `bad-witness-nonstandard`; the reviewed witness width is 86 against the relay limit of 80 (`STATE_METADATA_BYTES` in `packages/realization/src/state_codec.rs`, `TAPSCRIPT_STACK_ITEM_RELAY_LIMIT` in `packages/target-elements/src/transaction_form.rs`). The executor's relay-refusal path reports that layer only after its block fallback accepts, so the honest announcement reaches the declared relay boundary and is stopped there by the width rather than by another observed validation failure (`scripts/elements-native-executor.py`, `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`). That evidence answers the declared-boundary row and does not furnish schema 8's accepted identity and mined readback. `MaturityAcceptanceRoute` therefore keeps two routes: `RelayWitnessRestructure`, reopening the leaf and witness schedule to make the announcement relay-admissible, and `BlockLayerSubmissionSubject`, requiring a protocol revision that gives block submission its own subject (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`). Neither a policy relaxation nor the existing fallback relabelled as acceptance supplies the missing conjunct.

This record establishes a single recounted safety denominator, forty controlled first-party discharges, a total classification with typed outstanding obligations, a recomputed partial safety report, the exact-subject sweep and one admitted declared-boundary run. It settles the fourth exit conditionally on the sponsor-envelope row's internal inconsistency and leaves report completeness partial because 165 rows remain unanswered. It hands off the accepted-step, abstraction, completeness and environment obligations with their subjects named, while the findings below preserve the matrix, interface and capture constraints that another wave would otherwise have to rediscover; closing `T11-076` therefore closes this wave's evidence record rather than §23's acceptance obligation.

Wave 9 receives the accepted concrete step the forward clause needs, together with the predecessor and successor constructor projections at (`task:guide14-exec:wave9`); an accepted step must still be obtained through one of the two routes above, and the continuity obligation at (`rule:guide14-exec:bundle-continuity`) requires rejection when the two static subtrees differ even if metadata agrees. Wave 10 receives the class-2 responses and all three environment tests, the branch-indexed history and freshness obligations, and the outstanding root-history mutation registry at (`task:guide14-exec:wave10`); the class-3 marker and partial safety report establish none of those environmental facts. Whichever wave reopens the leaf receives `RelayAdmissibleWitnessSplitUnopened` from `packages/transaction/src/state_abi.rs`, with the witness widths, order, reconstruction, leaf commitments and resource accounting that the restructure must move together. The protocol receives the block-layer submission subject: both peers and the schema must change while historical captures remain parseable, because the current submission subject has bytes alone and forbids accepted-only fields on relay refusal (`packages/target-elements-conformance/src/protocol.rs`).

109. The vectors source census places the three `confidential_` entries after `fixture.rs` rather than in lexical order (`packages/vectors/meson.build`). `audit_census` converts declared paths into a set and compares membership, while also checking tracked modes, so it cannot detect list order (`packages/labels/src/census.rs`); a maintenance reorder would improve readability without changing membership or this wave's evidence.

110. Twenty-one pre-target rows declare `AbiConstructionRejection` while mutating `AbiLayout`, which no caller can offer to the validated ABI's private construction. `the_two_dispositions_partition_the_census` counts them under `LayoutIsNotAcceptedFromACaller`; changing finalized bytes instead would answer another layer and cannot discharge these rows as written (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`).

111. Three row names repeat across tables: `wrong-static-subtree`, `wrong-leaf-version` and `wrong-internal-key`. `every_row_is_named_once` checks the whole matrix by section and name, and the negative-half register uses that same pair because the latter two names repeat inside its waiting set; a name-only index would lose distinct subjects while still appearing to count unique names (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_negative_half.rs`).

112. The two successor lifecycle faults, `successor-remains-unannounced` and `successor-becomes-complete`, declare script-path rejection but name `RequiredExitMissingFromThePlan`, whose requirement boundary is `CompilerStatic`. `resolve_row` deliberately selects the class's boundary rather than the row's observation boundary, so resolution establishes a relation link and no published script-path refusal for that class (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`). Their native-run gaps remain open until an observation can answer the declared layer without borrowing the static obligation's result.

113. The 101 active declared rows meet on seventeen requirements in each representation; eighteen distinct declarations exist, with sponsor recognition inactive in the sponsorless case. `every_declared_row_resolves_in_the_sponsorless_case_of_every_representation` and `two_rows_share_a_requirement_only_by_sharing_their_declaration` assert these counts, so a requirement identity alone cannot identify which of its several row mutations a run answered (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`).

114. `collateral_for` assigns `RequireIntendedAndDependencyClosure` to every declared matrix row, while the plan's `collateral_policy` assigns `ReportAdditional` at compiler-static and external-evidence boundaries. The differing rows recount to 59: four `RequiredExitMissingFromThePlan`, thirteen `ExternalReportMissing`, thirteen `ExternalReportFailed` and twenty-nine `ExternalReportSubjectMismatch` declarations; the resolver tests compare relation, class and boundary but not this policy (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`, `packages/compiler/src/coverage.rs`). A resolved link therefore does not certify agreement of the collateral fields.

115. The constructibility declarations compress 35 differently worded matrix rows into twelve missing-report, seven failed-report and sixteen subject-mismatch classes. These are the three external-evidence classes that `MaturityMutationClass` resolves, so their vocabulary is coarser than §16's distinctions between keys, parity, leaves, nonces and linked programs; a concrete row's locator and control must continue to identify the fault after resolution (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`).

116. The fifteen report-layer rows span §16.3 six times, §16.10 twice and §16.12 seven times, counted from `report_fault` entries rather than inferred from a table title. Only nine have `ReportField` as locator: the six metadata rows name `SuccessorMetadataField`, even though their control is `CanonicalSafetyReport`. `each_control_shape_stands_only_on_rows_whose_subject_it_can_build` holds the incidence and `the_report_layer_requirements_are_the_matrixs_report_rows` selects by declared layer; a report control is therefore neither a function of the mutation subject alone nor confined to §16.12 (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`).

117. `MaximumLead` holds on positive rows two and five, because `stated_world` places `MaximumValidLead` at the window's upper endpoint and `CurrentCycleNearCheckedUpperDomain` at the largest current cycle whose upper endpoint still exists. The predicate reads that arithmetic while `PositiveRow::witness` keys the class to row two alone, so a class witness is not an assertion that only its named row satisfies the predicate (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs`).

118. `PositiveRow::ordinal` and `PositiveRow::position` are two explicit matches over the same fourteen ordered entries, differing by one. `the_registry_reads_its_row_names_from_the_safety_matrix` checks their agreement with the transcription, but the registry still holds two tables of one positional fact, so a future order change must move both (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs`).

119. Nonce leastness is a property of the search, not a validator over a supplied nonce: `CandidateStateConstructor::derive` starts at zero and retains every retryable rejection, while the first-party census carries the noncanonical-nonce row under `LeastnessIsAPropertyOfTheSearchNotOfAnOffer`. The real-link predicate test does not assert that its rejection list is nonempty, so its retryable clause can be vacuous when nonce zero succeeds; the same test supplies nonempty synthetic records to distinguish a repairable rejection, a nonretryable refusal, a skipped nonce and a selected nonce above the checked prefix (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`, `the_nonce_class_reads_the_links_own_retained_search` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs`).

120. `branch_hash` orders the two child hashes before commitment, so `caller-supplied-branch-order` and `source-order-dependent-tree` have no distinct malformed-order inhabitant at their declared constructor boundary. `each_mechanism_reason_stands_on_exactly_the_rows_it_names` carries both under `TheOfferedOrderIsNormalizedBeforeItIsCommitted`; changing committed bytes later would test a different subject (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

121. The constructor-side `wrong-static-subtree` and `extra-escape-leaf` rows ask for a subject comparison that the derivation cannot perform: it accepts the offered subtree and retains no independent expected subtree beside it. `constructor_reason` and `each_mechanism_reason_stands_on_exactly_the_rows_it_names` keep them under `TheOwnerHasNoRetainedObjectToCompareTheOfferAgainst`, which separates a missing comparison operand from a malformed subtree the validator can reject (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`, `packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs`).

122. `StateStaticLeaf` carries role, program and version, with no weight term, so `conflicting-leaf-weight` has nowhere to place its stated change. The mechanism test carries exactly that row under `TheTypedInputCarriesNoTermTheChangeNames`; adding another malformed leaf would not test a weight conflict (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

123. `MaturityAnnouncementRequest::new` owns no pre-target matrix discharge. Its request-level contradiction is sponsorless form with sponsor change, while the fourteen-member validator census drives semantic construction, authorization and the other entries that actually see the matrix's changes; the request constructor appears in the honest substrate, not as a validator member (`packages/transaction/src/state_request.rs`, `MaturityFirstPartyValidator` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

124. The first-party module's unqualified §4.2 and §4.3 references are Guide 13's negative-evidence and ABI-role rules, not Guide 14 sections. The live precedent has eleven case calls across `collection_time_cases` and `post_boundary_cases`, while the STATE module classifies 83 pre-target rows and discharges forty; citing the precedent supplies the two-run policy, not its census (`plans/guides/guide_thirteen.md`, `packages/vectors/src/live_first_party.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

125. The `reorder-fields` discharge reorders the metadata framing by moving the schema bytes before the domain separator. Swapping equal-width semantic amounts would still decode as another metadata value, so the fixed-width layout limits what the encoding validator can establish about field order; the case proves rejection of the stated framing mutation, not arbitrary semantic permutations (`ReorderTheMetadataFraming` and `metadata_discharges` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`, `packages/realization/src/state_codec.rs`).

126. `FirstPartyIndex::of` defaults a missing discharged case's class to the empty string through `unwrap_or_default`. The key currently exists because the discharges and class map come from the same `maturity_first_party_cases` census, but the index would conceal an internal disagreement instead of refusing it if those paths diverged (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

127. All six `MaturityDeferredOwner` members now name no row, and the empty set is asserted by `the_two_dispositions_partition_the_census`; the type is deliberately retained so a new pre-target row can distinguish a located but undriven owner from an impossible offered change. The `StateConstructorDerivation` member's documentation further corrects the earlier locating approximation: derivation owned none of its three former rows, because it fixes the branch side and retries the nonce itself, while the side check or a typed carried reason supplies their actual disposition (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

128. `observed_boundary` is a private module, so public explanations name its shared comparison in prose rather than relying on a public module path. The native module selectively re-exports `matches_boundary` and `observed_boundary` for the integration test, keeping one implementation available through the public consumer without exposing the entire module (`packages/vectors/src/lib.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_native.rs`).

129. Executor provenance is an externally stated eighth input, not a value the derivation can establish for itself. `stated_executor_provenance` returns `NotStatedByTheOperator` unless both executor tip and upstream base are supplied, and `the_plan_exposes_exactly_the_eight_inputs` explicitly exercises both unstated and stated inputs; an assertion about every historical gate's environment cannot be inferred from that test (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`). The committed run's provenance must therefore be assessed through its admitted report and capture, separately from the plan's default expectation.

130. `MaturityEvidenceCensus` has private fields and no constructor for a hand-built census, which is why the report tests project `CompletenessFacts` and keep editable answered/outstanding report fields for tamper checks. `WaitingRowCarriesNoRegisteredGap` is unreachable while `STILL_REQUIRED` and the classifier agree: `waiting_rows` raises it only for a `NativeRunRequired` row absent from the register, the exact disagreement `every_outstanding_row_is_registered` forbids (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_negative_half.rs`).

131. The live report's `VolatileField::ALL` has ten entries in its own §13.5 order, whereas the STATE report's eleven exclusions follow Guide 14 §14.9. The live vocabulary has no separate caller-selected-filesystem-path member and uses environment-value and test-fixture-material categories where the STATE list names ambient environment and private scalars, so copying the live enum would not reproduce the required partition (`packages/vectors/src/live_report.rs`, `MaturityVolatileField::ALL` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`, at (`rule:guide14-exec:report-canonicality`)).

132. The locator-absent arm of `StructuralSeparator` binding admits no negative row at this tip: `the_rows_that_point_nowhere_are_exactly_the_positive_fourteen` counts zero such negatives. The inhabited separator path is narrower, answering exactly the nine metadata-encoding subjects, with 183 other negative subjects rejecting it in `a_structural_separator_answers_only_the_subject_that_sits_between_fields` (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`).

133. The candidate-link entry returns `LinkRefusal::StateLink` around its `StateLinkRefusal`, whereas the linked-program check returns the STATE refusal directly. `supplied_constructor_commits_another_program` reads the wrapper and the two crossed-owner tests distinguish the entries, so matching only the inner class would erase which owning call actually refused (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_first_party.rs`).

134. §16.11's `state-and-sponsor-positions-exchanged` is the one measured control/subject inconsistency: its locator names a sponsor envelope and its canonical control is sponsorless. `CONTROL_AGAINST_SUBJECT` intentionally records that pair with count one; the sweep proves the incidence is faithfully transcribed and cannot prove an absent envelope exchangeable, which is the fourth exit's named condition (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`).

135. Rows admitting no mutant refusal number 66, not merely the 65 typed non-answers. The additional row is the positive sponsorless control, which declares relay policy yet departs from no accepted control; `no_refusal_binds_to_a_row_that_admits_none` and the 43-versus-42 boundary/binding counts keep its admitted declared-boundary observation distinct from a native mutant refusal (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`).

136. The native planner requires deployment identity and branch context as replay inputs; funding supplies neither a branch identifier nor a checkpoint. The integration run fixes `RUN_BRANCH` to a public identifier and checkpoint seven, records `current-root-freshness unestablished`, and compares those operands during replay, so its branch is caller-stated context rather than an independently established current-root fact (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_native.rs`, `MaturityNativeEvidence::from_transcript` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`).

137. `CeremonyId::Report` retains the legacy live-transfer test name by default, and `CaptureDestination::report_test_name` selects the maturity test without changing the ceremony roster. `shared_capture_names_preserve_the_legacy_roster` checks both names and rejects unrelated ones; a distinct maturity-report ceremony member is an alternative, but this capture uses the same report kind and explicitly changes its test-name operand (`packages/vectors/tests/common/mod.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_native.rs`).

138. The operator capture driver still requires `native-capture-schema 1`, while the shared writer emits schema 2 and the maturity driver requires 2. The committed operator capture is schema 1, so its existing admission is coherent with its bytes; a new operator capture through the current shared writer would meet the driver's stale schema check (`scripts/live-native-operator-capture.sh`, `scripts/live-native-maturity-capture.sh`, `packages/vectors/tests/common/mod.rs`, `packages/vectors/fixtures/native-operator-run-of-record/`).

139. A register or census fact can be pinned outside its owning module. `the_register_no_longer_requires_the_sponsorless_target_run` lives in the planner, `the_census_figures_are_unmoved_by_the_binding_readers` in the evidence module, and `the_completeness_is_partial_at_this_tip` in the report; all now reflect admission. A change moving such a fact must locate every test that names it, because a file-scoped search of the register alone misses these independently useful checks (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`).

140. The operator importer exposes deployment network and genesis identities through validated report readers. The maturity importer compares against `operator.report().network_id()` and `operator.report().genesis_id()` rather than duplicated literals, so the cross-run equality is between admitted facts and cannot drift into two independent constants (`packages/vectors/src/live_corpus_native_operator.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`).

141. The maturity capture has an empty `handshake-framework-revision` despite populated intended-executed-tip and binary-reported-revision fields; the operator capture's framework field is populated. `framework_revision` attempts to read the framework checkout and can return no value, so the evidence establishes a missing framework stamp in this run, not an adapter that never stamps one (`packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`, `packages/vectors/fixtures/native-operator-run-of-record/`, `scripts/elements-native-executor.py`).

142. Empty byte fields in the committed maturity capture retain their final separator space, including the empty framework field, upstream base, funding request bytes and funding response details. Those spaces are part of the immutable admitted byte stream and its hashes, so a whitespace check applied to fixture additions flags bytes that source-only checks exclude; normalizing them would invalidate the archive rather than repair source formatting (`packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`, `every_file_byte_flip_stops_at_its_outer_layer` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`).

### Wave-9 plan findings · `sec:phase6:wave9-findings`

The Wave-9 opening study was a read-only source study with no gate run, and its citations were self-audited; the citations below were located again in the tree at the tip this record opens from, the source figures were recounted rather than carried, and the totals are the wave's baseline rather than evidence its planned producer has already established. The study's independent hash and transaction-decoder calculations are distinguished below from the recorded fields this source audit can read; `T11-094` owes their repository recomputation.

143. Finding 119's leastness gap has a concrete nonzero archived subject: the predecessor-metadata witness in the admitted submission carries representation nonce 1, and its successor-nonce item carries 0 (`packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`, loaded by `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs:37-40`, with metadata layout in `packages/realization/src/state_codec.rs:132-158` and witness roles in `packages/transaction/src/state_signing.rs:935-973`). The opening study's independent hash recount found that this predecessor at nonce 0 gives a metadata-leaf hash above the witnessed static root, which `StateBranchSide::check` refuses; that arithmetic is not recomputed by this record and the constructor-evidence bite `T11-094` must recompute the lower-candidate rejection prefix (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs:510-519,865-911`). The production-oracle test `the_production_golden_is_the_least_nonce_satisfying_the_branch_order` explicitly uses a zero golden nonce and an empty lower-nonce loop, while `late_candidate_records_every_retry_in_nonce_order` supplies a nonempty scripted prefix; a nonzero archived representation therefore supplies the real-arithmetic subject that the zero golden cannot exercise (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_constructor_tests.rs:362-418,1157-1186`), without promoting the study's calculation into a repository test result.

144. The registry's sponsorless fixture is not the native scenario: `stated_world` uses cycle 5 and `SECOND_WINDOW` 3–5 to request 9, whereas `PublishedSignerHeld` uses bounds 4–6 and the native planner requests earliest plus one capped at latest, hence 10; the node-free candidate names that same request explicitly (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs:151-155,882-940`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs:380-395,633-648`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs:533-638`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_abi.rs:87-116`). A shared row label used as an expected-value lookup would hide the mismatch. `semantic_case` instead calls `announce_maturity` with the actual predecessor, bounds and request, so ruling 71 can use that producer without importing the registry fixture's different world (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_fixture.rs:991-1026`).

145. `CandidateStateConstructor::continuity` compares static root, internal key and leaf version only, while `state_bundle_continuity` compares the full retained `StateStaticSubtree`; its documentation explains why the root is insufficient, since leaf identities are not committed (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs:1065-1076`, `packages/linker/src/state_bundle.rs:373-411`). The leaf preimage contains version and framed program bytes, not descriptor identity (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs:197-203`). Root equality therefore cannot substitute for retained-descriptor equality, and neither comparison proves an unknown hidden subtree contains only declared leaves; the exact operands must accompany the evidence rather than be inferred from a common commitment.

146. The public view validates an offered metadata/nonce/program triple reconstructed under the bundle without comparing it to the first retained metadata tuple, while finalization's executing leaf and signing's predecessor parity read the first retained constructor (`packages/transaction/src/state_view.rs:430-465`, `packages/transaction/src/state_finalize.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/state_signing.rs:935-1000`). This is a source-identified missing comparison, not a reproduced failure: the paths read different sources, but no executed coherent-alternate-instance case here shows that they produce a reachable bad spend. Ruling 77 consequently requires the projector's retained-context comparison and a characterization test before any separately ruled transaction API change.

147. The submission does not itself carry the spent output's program, value and asset, a branch or network identity, a nonce-search budget, target policy, or a descriptor for hidden support leaves. The admitted exchanges supply funding facts, the view retains spent and branch context, and the bundle retains policy and constructor recipes (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs:221-275`, `packages/transaction/src/state_view.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/state_finalize.rs:674-701`, `packages/linker/src/state_bundle.rs:501-515,672-713`). Those are explicit projection inputs rather than facts recoverable from the submitted transaction alone; finding 136 already records the branch operands' caller-stated status. The successor's derived control recipe is likewise reconstruction evidence, not an observed successor-spend witness (`packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs:983-1016`).

148. The maturity linker rebuilds the static tree as identity 0 and one linked announcement leaf, although `StateStaticSubtree` admits branches and support leaves (`packages/linker/src/state_bundle.rs:974-990`, `packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs:340-410`). Both concrete maturity sources pass through that singleton linker, so neither exercises hidden-subtree reconstruction with support leaves (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_native.rs:533-590`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_abi.rs`). The archive's predecessor control is framed as 65 bytes, one version/parity byte and one internal key followed by one sibling; the announcement control recipe appends the metadata-leaf commitment to the static leaf's path, which is empty for this singleton (`packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs:37-40`, `packages/tapscript/src/state_constructor.rs:983-1016`). This confines the observed path's coverage rather than claiming that a support-leaf descriptor was recovered.

149. The eight mutation subjects selected for constructor-continuity work identify 55 matrix rows, recounted from `Loc` entries in `MATURITY_SAFETY_ROWS` through the locator-to-subject mapping: StaticSubtree 10, BranchOrder 3, Nonce 9, Tweak 4, ControlBlock 9, LinkedProgram 5, PredecessorMetadata 4 and SuccessorMetadata 11 (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_safety.rs`). The 24-member locator and subject vocabularies are not row counts. `CONTROL_AGAINST_SUBJECT` splits SuccessorMetadata into six safety-report controls and five sponsorless controls; selecting all eleven as transaction mutations would therefore cross a declared carrier boundary. Finding 111's section-plus-name identity remains necessary because names repeat across tables, and ruling 75 adds cases against that one matrix rather than a second denominator.

150. The fifteen-member standing vocabulary already counted `ConstructorContinuityObserved` as answered, but neither `classify` nor the constructor registry produced it: classification selected first-party, native, report-required or typed non-answer standings, and the mutation registry, the type the tree now names `MaturityMutationRegistry`, initialized an empty constructor mutation list under `ConstructorProjectionsAreNotPublished` at the tip the Wave-9 record opened from (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs:358-414,484-520,985-1139,1746-1777` at that tip). `T11-096` retired that constructor registry on `0.6.238-dev`, replacing it with typed constructor material and leaving `RootHistory` the registry's one kind. The census assertion stood at zero for constructor-continuity observations (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs:2094-2102` at that tip). This was a missing validated producer rather than an absent vocabulary member, and ruling 73 had to preserve the standing's accepted-byte boundary when it replaced the registry's material representation.

151. The negative-half staging prose describes successor metadata as an output field, a fee output in the sponsorless shape, and spent asset and amount as writable input fields (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_negative_half.rs:358-399,450-487`). The landed carrier instead has one STATE output and no fee output, represents metadata through the witness and constructor-derived output program, and retains spent-output facts separately (`packages/transaction/src/state_construct.rs:235-280`, `packages/transaction/src/state_signing.rs:935-973`, `packages/transaction/src/state_finalize.rs:674-701`). A future continuity mutator must identify that actual carrier and coherently rebuild commitments and signature where the intended comparison requires them; a textual field substitution following the staging prose would not isolate the claimed fault. This record leaves the drifted prose unchanged.

152. The archive's submission records 1694 request bytes and transaction weight 2084; its recorded response also has null accepted identity and mined readback, so measured size is no acceptance witness (`packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-run-of-record/`, loaded by `packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`). The opening study's decoder census gives a 130-byte stripped encoding and nine witness widths of 1, 4, 8, 32, 86, 1, 64, 1286 and 65, with the announcement leaf and predecessor control last. The first seven widths agree with the node-free witness census in `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_abi.rs:366-389`; the full archived decoder census remains the study's calculation until `T11-094` recounts it through the repository decoder in `packages/transaction/src/bytes.rs:1388-1452`. Protected finalized bytes omit this witness and cannot replace the submitted source in the projection (`packages/transaction/src/state_finalize.rs:674-701`).

153. Finding 131 already distinguishes the live report's ten volatile categories from STATE's eleven; the additional reuse boundary is the report role and subject. `LiveSafetyReportRole` and `MaturitySafetyReportRole` identify safety evidence, and live subject fields describe live transfers, so the reusable boundary and layer types, observed-layer comparison and exact-subject wrappers cannot make that role a continuity role (`packages/vectors/src/live_report.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_report.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/matrix.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/observed_boundary.rs`). Ruling 72 therefore reuses the validation discipline and STATE exclusion types while giving continuity its own independently validated report.

Row `T11-091` is DONE on `0.6.229-dev`. Its gate asked for the opening record and resolving labels; rulings 70–78 at (`rule:phase6:wave9-rulings`), findings 143–153 here, the backlog rows and the register's Wave-9 paragraph supply that record, and the documentation and `tripod-labels` gates check its citations.

Row `T11-092` is DONE on `0.6.230-dev`. Its gate asked for both semantic sides, real nonce-search arithmetic, static-tree distinctions and derived control paths; `real_maturity_transition_reconstructs_both_sides_and_separates_static_from_semantic_agreement`, `real_nonce_search_checks_a_pinned_nonzero_prefix_and_exhausts_one_attempt_short`, `real_commitments_forget_static_order_and_identity_but_detect_program_changes` and `real_transition_control_recipes_change_only_outer_sibling_and_output_parity` cover the constructor cases, while `equal_static_roots_do_not_establish_retained_descriptor_continuity` checks retained identity (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_constructor_tests.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_bundle_tests.rs`).

Row `T11-093` is DONE on `0.6.231-dev`. Its gate asked for byte-exact predecessor and successor projection, funded-predecessor coherence and separate static and semantic comparisons; `submitted_bytes_round_trip_exactly`, `wrong_funded_outpoint_is_refused`, `retained_metadata_disagreement_is_refused_first`, `predecessor_program_comparison_names_both_operands`, `static_root_mutation_stops_before_transition` and `archived_source_projects` exercise those boundaries (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`).

Row `T11-094` is DONE on `0.6.232-dev`. Its gate asked for checked nonce prefixes, tweak and parity evidence, observed and derived control paths, and coherent negative axes; `archived_prefix_is_checked_hash_by_hash`, `tweak_evidence_recomputes_both_sides`, `controls_keep_observed_and_derived_separate`, `cross_bundle_successors_refuse_output_with_valid_control`, `wrong_semantic_successors_refuse_output_with_valid_control` and `admissible_predecessor_substitution_refuses_retained_context` exercise those claims (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`).

Row `T11-095` is DONE on `0.6.235-dev`. Its gate asked for an independently validated and canonical continuity report; `independent_derivations_render_identical_bytes`, `every_item_is_mutated_or_has_an_explicit_type_barrier`, `independent_static_and_semantic_results_keep_their_own_claims` and `key_order_cardinality_and_residuals_are_canonical` check validation, distinct claims and rendering (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity_report.rs`).

Row `T11-096` is DONE on `0.6.238-dev`. Its gate asked for typed material and exact-row association without changing standings; `present_material_associates_exact_records_without_moving_standings`, `absent_material_preserves_the_entire_partition_and_has_no_records` and `the_census_buckets_are_the_figures_the_inputs_recompute` check that boundary (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`).

Row `T11-097` is DONE on `0.6.247-dev`. Its gate asked for public-builder reconstruction through projection and report over every concrete source, with independent negatives and explicit acceptance scope; `accepted_variable_archive_projects_both_constructors_and_transition`, `each_request_uses_the_predecessor_and_lead_window_rule`, `independently_rebuilt_reports_render_identical_source_bindings`, `accepted_archive_projection_and_report_keep_acceptance_outstanding` and the eight one-axis refusal tests cover the integration (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_continuity.rs`).

Row `T11-099` is DONE on the label gate, this record. Its gate asked for the Wave-9 dispositions, exits, register clauses and residual owners over landed evidence; the documentation and `tripod-labels` gates check this closure and close `T11-091` without assigning this record a patch tag.

The eight deliverables at (`task:guide14-exec:wave9`) have distinct landings: predecessor and successor constructor projection and static-subtree equality on `0.6.231-dev`; canonical nonce, tweak, parity and control-path evidence and coherent stale and cross-bundle negatives on `0.6.232-dev`; the validated continuity report on `0.6.235-dev`; and the end-to-end accepted-source integration on `0.6.247-dev`. The constructor characterization on `0.6.230-dev` and typed material association on `0.6.238-dev` supply their supporting gates (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_constructor_tests.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity_report.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_evidence.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_continuity.rs`).

The first exit at (`task:guide14-exec:wave9`) — accepted target bytes reconstruct both constructors — is MET. The second corpus strictly admits the accepted identity and exact mined readback through `variable_archive_admits_established_identity_and_exact_mined_readback` on `0.6.245-dev`; `accepted_variable_archived_bytes_project_with_complete_byte_identity` on `0.6.245-dev` and `accepted_variable_archive_projects_both_constructors_and_transition` on `0.6.247-dev` reconstruct both sides over those bytes (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_continuity.rs`). A host projection alone establishes neither target acceptance, row standing nor root freshness.

The second exit — static continuity and metadata continuity both hold — is MET. `accepted_variable_archive_projects_both_constructors_and_transition` checks the static root, control block, retained descriptor and constructor alongside the semantic transition and reconstruction over the same accepted bytes; `accepted_variable_archived_bytes_project_with_complete_byte_identity` checks their byte identity and both comparison results (`packages/vectors/tests/maturity_continuity.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`). The comparisons establish neither a classifier standing nor freshness of the caller-stated branch context.

The third exit — neither continuity result is inferred from the other — is MET. `independent_static_and_semantic_results_keep_their_own_claims` keeps `StaticRootBinding`, `StaticControlBinding`, `DescriptorResult` and `ConstructorResult` apart from `TransitionAgreement` and `ReconstructionAgreement`; `every_item_is_mutated_or_has_an_explicit_type_barrier` makes altered claims refuse at their own report items (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity_report.rs`). Independent recomputation does not itself establish target acceptance, row standing or root freshness.

The seventh conjunct at (`gate:guide14-exec:exit`) is CONDITIONALLY MET. The variable-schedule sponsorless announcement, with one input and one output, was accepted into the target mempool and mined at height 5; `variable_archive_admits_established_identity_and_exact_mined_readback` admits its target identity and exact readback on `0.6.245-dev` (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`, `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-variable-run-of-record/RUN-REPORT`). The executor disclosed `-minrelaytxfee=0` and `-blockmintxfee=0`; relayability under a positive fee floor is the deployment or Wave-12 condition, and `T11-111` owns entry of that acceptance into the row classifier.

The Wave-9 forward-comparison share of `G14C-02` is discharged on `0.6.245-dev` and `0.6.247-dev`: `variable_archive_admits_established_identity_and_exact_mined_readback`, `accepted_variable_archived_bytes_project_with_complete_byte_identity` and `accepted_variable_archive_projects_both_constructors_and_transition` bind the accepted concrete step to both exact-byte projections and their compared successor (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_continuity.rs`). `G14C-02` remains OPEN for the typed abstraction relation, the general forward-simulation theorem and supported-step completeness under named representability assumptions with Waves 2, 10 and 13.

The Wave-9 codec and projection share of `G14C-14` is discharged on `0.6.230-dev`, `0.6.232-dev`, `0.6.245-dev` and `0.6.247-dev`, while the row remains OPEN. `decode_after_encode_is_identity` and `encode_after_decode_is_byte_identical` test the two codec directions; `distinct_pairs_encode_distinctly` tests semantic-value/nonce injectivity; `field_slices_match_canonical_bytes_and_exact_differences` and `replacing_field_slice_changes_only_its_projection` test the slicing law; `a_tagged_hash_is_the_tag_digest_twice_and_then_the_message` and `a_tag_separates_domains` test tagged hashing; and `accepted_variable_archived_bytes_project_with_complete_byte_identity`, `real_commitments_forget_static_order_and_identity_but_detect_program_changes` and `equal_static_roots_do_not_establish_retained_descriptor_continuity` keep byte, root and hidden-content claims distinct (`packages/realization/src/tests/state_codec_tests.rs`, `packages/target-elements-conformance/src/tests/constructor_tests.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`, `packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_constructor_tests.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_bundle_tests.rs`). `tweak_evidence_recomputes_both_sides` checks the recorded premise count, but no test pins each collision and second-preimage premise to its exact leaf, branch and tweak domain; that remains Wave 9's clause, carried by `T11-112`.

The remaining obligations have owners: `T11-111` owns accepted-byte classifier standings and the resulting census and register equality; `T11-112` owns the six premise-to-domain test pairings; deployment or Wave 12 owns positive-fee-floor relayability; Wave 10 owns root freshness, branch-stale evidence and branch-indexed history; Wave 12 owns resource closure; artifact promotion requires its own gate; and the executable-model and typed-adapter semantic producers remain outstanding under ruling 71 at (`rule:phase6:wave9-rulings`). The accepted comparison answers none of those obligations by implication.

This record establishes Wave 9's three exits over accepted, exactly read-back target bytes, states the fee-floor condition on §23's seventh conjunct, and advances the register only through the tested projection and codec clauses. `T11-111` carries the separate classifier change required by ruling 73 and `T11-112` the premise-to-domain test.

Wave 10 receives caller-stated branch context for freshness, competing-transition and recovery work at (`task:guide14-exec:wave10`); Wave 12 receives the positive-fee-floor and resource questions, while `T11-111` receives the accepted-byte standings and `T11-112` receives the premise-to-domain test. The executable-model and typed-adapter producers remain outstanding under ruling 71, and artifact promotion retains its own gate.

### Route closure: the relay-admissible witness · `sec:phase6:route-closure`

The constant-elision route carries the canonical predecessor metadata's contiguous 53-byte variable region in a seven-role witness and reconstructs its 25-byte header and eight reserved zeros in a seven-instruction, 40-byte prologue before the unchanged authentication checks; the historical whole-item widths are [1, 4, 8, 32, 86, 1, 64] and the variable widths are [1, 4, 8, 32, 53, 1, 64] against an 80-byte initial-item policy, with the sixth linked resource dimension measuring a greatest initial argument of 86 and 64 respectively (`packages/target-elements/src/tests/transaction_form_tests.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_resource_tests.rs`). The variable leaf has seven pushed keys at sixteen relocation sites, nine changed and seven kept in the second deployment; the fifteen historical sites keep their keys, three positions stay fixed and twelve shift by the prologue (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_relocate_tests.rs`). The two schedules have fourteen independently checked constructor goldens and eighteen vectors goldens; the semantic recipe's selected nonces are 0 and 0 for the whole schedule and 1 and 5 for the variable schedule (`packages/tapscript/src/tests/state_constructor_tests.rs`, `packages/vectors/tests/maturity_closure.rs`). All three historical requests replay byte-identically and cross-schedule replay refuses (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`). The second immutable corpus at `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-variable-run-of-record/` binds its report address through `MATURITY_VARIABLE_RUN_ADDRESS`, its manifest digest through `MATURITY_VARIABLE_MANIFEST_SHA256` and its capture digest through `VARIABLE_PINS.ceremony_sha256()`, each checked against the embedded file bytes (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`, `packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-variable-run-of-record/MANIFEST.sha256`). Its third exchange is accepted with the target identity printed by `response-target-identity`, the witness identity and block hash in the admitted `accepted-witness-identity` and `accepted-block` fields, and exact mined readback at height 5; the 1,701-byte submitted transaction has witness widths [1, 4, 8, 32, 53, 1, 64, 1326, 65], requests cycle 10 and projects predecessor nonce 1 and successor nonce 0, with its complete submitted-byte SHA-256 identity recounted by `sha256sum` over the decoded request bytes and asserted by `accepted_variable_archived_bytes_project_with_complete_byte_identity` in `packages/vectors/src/maturity_continuity.rs`. The executor records its framework revision as the intended tip and uses `-minrelaytxfee=0` and `-blockmintxfee=0`, so this is accepted evidence in that disclosed environment (`packages/vectors/fixtures/maturity-variable-run-of-record/RUN-REPORT`, `scripts/elements-native-executor.py`).

Row `T11-100` is DONE on `0.6.233-dev` and closes here on `0.6.245-dev`. Its gate asked for the ratified route, its ten-row sequence and a closure under `T11-109`; ADR-025, ruling 79 at (`rule:phase6:wave9-rulings`), the backlog dispositions below and the documentation and label gate supply that sequence and closure.

Row `T11-101` is DONE on `0.6.234-dev`. Its gate asked that classification and encoding agree without changing strict decoding; `STATE_METADATA_LAYOUT`, `STATE_METADATA_VARIABLE_RANGE`, `state_metadata_variable_region` and `rebuild_state_metadata` derive and test the 53-byte span while the domain, schema and reserved-field refusals remain (`packages/realization/src/state_codec.rs`, `packages/realization/src/tests/state_codec_tests.rs`).

Row `T11-102` is DONE on `0.6.236-dev`. Its gate asked that retained-schedule replay preserve the archive and refuse both new whole-item deployment and cross-schedule replay; the three archived requests replay byte-identically under the historical schedule, and the alternate leaf's transcript refuses at step zero (`packages/vectors/src/maturity_corpus.rs`, `packages/vectors/src/maturity_closure.rs`).

Row `T11-103` is DONE on `0.6.239-dev`. Its gate asked for coordinated policy, lowering, prologue, symbol, loader and form review; the variable schedule carries 53 bytes at witness index four, the seven-instruction prologue restores the historical authentication entry, `MetadataHeader` is width-checked, and typed refusals cover invalid layout and populated item widths (`packages/target-elements/src/tests/transaction_form_tests.rs`, `packages/tapscript/src/state_announcement.rs`, `packages/linker/src/tests/state_symbol_tests.rs`, `packages/transaction/src/tests/state_signing_tests.rs`).

Row `T11-104` is DONE on `0.6.240-dev`. Its gate asked for initial-argument admission beside the execution bound; 80 passes and 81 fails the initial-item comparator, the whole item's 86 is refused, all seven variable arguments fit with 64 greatest, and script, control and five unmeasured resource dimensions remain outside that comparison (`packages/linker/src/tests/state_resource_tests.rs`).

Row `T11-105` is DONE on `0.6.241-dev`. Row `T11-109` is DONE on the label gate, this record.
